*Kouki Yuki,Jie Zeng,Kyoko Ogawa,Ryunosuke Ikeda,Yohei Kobashi,Takeshi Kojima,Ikuya Yamada,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于执行监督和偏好强化学习（GRPO）的多语言到多语言代码翻译方法。首先从可验证的Python种子程序扩展出多语言执行验证代码池，基于该代码池生成翻译候选并通过执行结果标注偏好，训练奖励模型后对25×24共600个语言对进行优化。同时引入HumanEval-X++基准评估广泛多语言翻译能力，4B模型在HumanEval-X++上平均提升13%，中端语言提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代码翻译主要聚焦于少数流行语言（如C++、Java、Python），忽略了众多其他编程语言之间的多对多翻译场景。该场景下并行监督数据稀疏，导致模型产生看似合理但无法执行的翻译结果，迫切需要一种能够处理多语言稀疏监督的可靠训练方法。

Method: 1) 数据生成：从可验证的Python种子程序扩展出多语言执行验证代码池；2) 偏好构建：基于代码池让基础LLM生成跨语言翻译候选，通过执行结果标注偏好；3) 奖励模型训练：使用偏好数据训练评估跨语言翻译质量的奖励模型；4) 策略优化：利用GRPO在600个有向语言对（25×24）上以奖励模型为信号优化基础LLM；5) 基准构建：引入HumanEval-X++执行基准覆盖广泛的多对多语言空间。

Result: 在Qwen-3.5 4B和9B模型上评估，方法在HumanEval-X++及现有基准上均取得一致增益。4B模型在HumanEval-X++上平均提升13%，其中中端语言提升达21%。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了数据生成、训练和基准测试的可靠框架，为多对多编程语言翻译质量的进一步自举提升铺平了道路。

Abstract: Code translation must preserve executable behavior across many programming languages, yet neural code translation has largely focused on a few popular languages such as C++, Java, and Python. This leaves a niche, many-to-many setting where parallel supervision is sparse, producing plausible but non-executable translations. We address this setting with preference-based reinforcement learning driven by execution-based supervision. Our pipeline firstly expands verifiable seed Python programs into a multilingual pool of execution-validated codes. Using the pool, a base LLM generates translation candidates across language pairs, which we label by their execution outcomes. The resulting preferences are used to train a reward model that scores cross-language translation quality. Finally, we optimize our base LLMs with GRPO over 600 directed language pairs (25 x 24) using the reward model as a signal. To evaluate the niche translation capability, we introduce HumanEval-X++, an execution-based benchmark that extends HumanEval-X to a broad many-to-many language space. We evaluate our approach using Qwen-3.5 4B and 9B models. On HumanEval-X++ and existing benchmarks, it yields consistent gains over the untrained baselines. In particular, the 4B model achieves an average improvement of 13% across all languages on HumanEval-X++, with a gain of 21% on mid-tier languages. Our study establishes a reliable approach of data generation, training, and benchmarking, paving the way toward further bootstrapping the quality of many-to-many translation for programming languages.

</details>


### [85] [Scaling Creative Writing Beyond Story-Centric Data with Attribute-Guided Genre Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13947)
*Hwan Chang,Yongil Kim,Heuiyeen Yeen,Yireun Kim,Jinsik Lee,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种属性引导的体裁扩展框架，用于超越故事生成、规模化构建多样创意写作数据。该框架通过分离主题广度与体裁形式控制，利用人类撰写的故事提示作为创意种子，结合人工策划的体裁属性来强制执行结构、风格和格式规范，构建了覆盖13种创意体裁的50K样本语料库（Multi-Genre Collection）。实验表明，在该数据上微调的模型在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的高质量创意写作数据主要由故事类数据主导，这限制了模型遵循多样化创意格式的结构和功能惯例的能力。作者希望通过可控的体裁扩展来弥补这一空白。

Method: 提出属性引导的体裁扩展框架：(1) 将主题广度与体裁形式控制分离；(2) 使用人类撰写的故事提示作为创意种子；(3) 利用人工策划的体裁属性强制结构、风格和格式规范；(4) 组合这些输入提示强LLM生成体裁一致的查询-响应对，并经过质量过滤。最终构建Multi-Genre Collection，涵盖13种创意体裁共50K示例。

Result: 在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断中，微调模型一致超越基础模型、写作特化基线及现有写作语料上训练的模型。体裁数量消融实验表明，受控的体裁扩展而非仅故事中心扩展是稳健创意写作能力的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 受控的体裁扩展比单纯的故事中心扩展更能驱动稳健的创意写作能力，属性引导的体裁扩展框架是超越故事生成、规模化创意写作数据的有效途径。

Abstract: High-quality creative writing data for large language models (LLMs) remains dominated by story-centric data, limiting models' ability to follow the structural and functional conventions of diverse creative formats. We propose an attribute-guided genre expansion framework for scaling creative writing data beyond story generation. By separating thematic breadth from genre-form control, our framework leverages human-authored story prompts as diverse creative seeds, while utilizing manually curated genre attributes to enforce distinct structural, stylistic, and formatting conventions. We combine these to prompt strong LLMs for genre-faithful query-response pairs, which are then quality-filtered. Applying this framework, we construct the Multi-Genre Collection, a 50K-example corpus spanning 13 creative genres, including story, rap, lyrics, scripts, game design, character design, and other creative formats. Experiments across out-of-distribution writing benchmarks and held-out genre diagnostics demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our data consistently surpass not only base models and writing-specialized baselines, but also models trained on existing writing corpora. Genre-count ablations further indicate that controlled genre expansion, rather than story-centric scaling alone, is a key driver of robust creative writing capability.

</details>


### [86] [Repair, Not Improvement: Decomposing Constrained Decoding in Tool-Call Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13959)
*Janghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数调用中的工具弃权（tool abstention）场景，即约束生成（constrained generation）在函数调用中的表现。通过在字节相同的提示词上分离语法的两个功能（决定生成停止位置vs决定可发射的token），对0.6B到4B的开源模型在英韩匹配项上进行评估，发现先前的对比结果在弃权场景下多为负效应（最多-29.5点），与无约束解码器相比没有正效应。最终结论是约束生成对工具弃权问题的帮助有限，其恢复的仅是形式而非内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的约束生成研究明确将函数调用排除在其范围之外，并警告不要将格式约束的结论外推到编码正确性要求的场景。工具弃权正是这种情形最尖锐的例子：枚举（enum）不改变答案措辞，只缩小答案集合，而不调用任何工具是首先被丢弃的情况。作者希望测量这个被排除在外的案例。

Method: 使用三个条件、一个字节相同的提示词来分离语法的两种不同功能：一个决定生成何时停止，另一个决定哪些token可以被发射。对0.6B到4B规模的开源权重模型，在匹配的英语和韩语项目上进行评估，使得语言比较在项目内进行。

Result: 在六个单元格中，先前的对比在弃权任务上有四个为负效应且置信区间排除零（最差为-29.5个百分点），没有任何正效应的区间排除零。在最小的韩语模型上，停止token成本为-20.0，枚举返回+19.5，两者合计为-0.5。在修复的698个弃权中，545个没有可读答案，0个是被评分者拒绝的判断。在需要工具的项目上结果始终为正。两个预注册的语言假设均未通过。

Conclusion: 约束生成在工具弃权场景下并未带来预期的正面效果，其恢复的主要是形式（如可读性）而非实质内容（评分者认可的正确响应）。停止条件对中小型模型在弃权任务上构成显著挑战，而枚举约束只能部分补偿。两个预注册的语言假设均失败，表明语言差异未按预期方向表现。

Abstract: Function calling is what the recent accounting of constrained generation explicitly sets aside: it finds the decoder's contribution small for format constraints, then warns in its Section 7 against extrapolating where a constraint encodes a correctness requirement, and names function calling as one. Tool abstention is that case at its sharpest: an enum leaves the wording of an answer alone and narrows the set of answers there are, and declining to call anything is the first it drops. We measure the excluded case. Three conditions over one byte-identical prompt separate a grammar's two jobs: it fixes where generation stops as well as which tokens may be emitted. We evaluate open-weight models from 0.6B to 4B on matched English and Korean items, so the language comparison is made within item. Against an unconstrained decoder, prior work's contrast is negative on abstention in four of six cells with intervals excluding zero, worst -29.5 points, and positive with an interval excluding zero in none. The total is a sum with opposite signs: on the smallest model in Korean the stop token costs -20.0, the enum returns +19.5, and the two leave -0.5. What it recovers is form: of 698 abstentions repaired, 545 had no readable answer and 0 were judgements the scorer refused. On tool-needed items it is positive throughout; abstention leads because it is the preregistered measure, and the pooled number being kinder to the intervention makes moving to it worse rather than better. Both preregistered language claims fail.

</details>


### [87] [Batch-wise Adaptive Pruning: Periodic Neuron Activation-Aware Weight Pruning for Language Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14003)
*Yongmin Kim,Shota Takashiro,Yusuke Iwasawa,Takeshi Kojima,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大推理模型（LRMs）批量推理场景的训练-free自适应剪枝方法。该方法用周期性top-k选择替代阈值选择，并引入激活记忆机制累积重要性分数，解决了批量推理中共享剪枝掩码导致的分布偏移和稀疏率漂移问题，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B等模型上较此前方法提升39.7个百分点准确率，并实现1.40倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRMs通过长链式推理获得强大性能，但推理计算成本高。生产环境中批量推理至关重要，然而现有训练-free自适应剪枝方法在批量推理场景下性能严重退化——因为批次共享单一剪枝掩码，激活聚合后分布发生偏移，离线校准的阈值不再匹配，导致实际稀疏率漂移、推理任务准确率崩塌。

Method: 提出一种专为LRMs批量推理设计的训练-free自适应剪枝方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 用周期性top-k选择替代阈值选择，对聚合激活分布的变化不敏感，且每个更新周期只执行一次选择而非每个token都执行，保持加速效果；(2) 基于重要神经元在长推理生成过程中周期性重新激活的观察，引入激活记忆机制，跨更新阶段累积重要性分数，确保周期性复现的神经元被保留。

Result: 在多样化的推理基准上，该方法在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上、batch size为4、目标稀疏率50%的条件下，平均准确率较此前最先进的自适应剪枝方法提升39.7个百分点；在50%实际稀疏率下达到相对于稠密推理的1.40倍加速。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了批量推理场景下自适应剪枝的关键挑战（阈值偏移与分布不匹配），并提出了有效的解决方案。通过top-k周期选择和激活记忆累积，在批量推理中同时保持了高准确率和推理加速，为LRMs在生产环境中的高效部署提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex tasks through extended chain-of-thought generation, but incur substantial computational costs during inference. In production settings, batched inference is essential for high throughput, yet the existing training-free adaptive pruning methods we evaluate severely degrade in this regime. Because a batch must share a single pruning mask, these methods aggregate activations across samples and then apply threshold-based selection; the threshold, calibrated offline on unaggregated activations, no longer matches the aggregated distribution, so the realized sparsity ratio drifts and accuracy on reasoning tasks collapses under batched inference.
  In this work, we propose a training-free adaptive pruning method designed specifically for batched inference in LRMs, built on two components. First, we replace threshold-based selection with periodic top-k selection over the aggregated importance scores, which is unaffected by the shift that aggregation induces in the activation distribution, and which runs selection once per update period rather than at every token, preserving the speedup. Second, based on the observation that important neurons re-fire periodically during long reasoning generation, we introduce an activation memory that accumulates importance across update phases so that recurring neurons are retained.
  Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art adaptive pruning method by 39.7 percentage points in average accuracy at batch size 4 with 50% target sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, and reaches 1.40x speedup over dense inference at 50% actual sparsity.

</details>


### [88] [S2Dialog: Multimodal Dialogue Retrieval with Semantic and Acoustic-Style Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14029)
*Xueqi Wang,Zhigang Wang,Runqing Zhang,Zhenqi Jia,Junfeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出S2Dialog，一个统一的对话级语义-风格检索框架，用于从多模态对话库中检索与目标对话在文本语义和声学对话风格上相似的对话。该框架包含对话级文本检索器和对话级声学检索器，并通过对话级文本-声学对比学习增强多模态对齐，在DailyTalk数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话检索方法大多局限于话语级别或单模态匹配，难以捕捉整段对话的全局语义连贯性和风格一致性。然而这类对话级检索对于情感识别、口语对话系统、对话语音合成等任务至关重要，因此需要一种能够同时建模对话级文本语义与声学风格的统一检索框架。

Method: S2Dialog包含两个核心组件：(1) 对话级文本检索器（Dialogue-level Textual Retriever）与对话级声学检索器（Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever），分别将对话的文本和声学模态编码为对话级表示；(2) 对话级文本-声学对比学习（Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning），用于对齐语义和风格相似的对话并区分不相关的对话，从而增强多模态检索能力。

Result: 在多模态对话数据集DailyTalk上进行了大量实验，S2Dialog取得了优异的检索性能。

Conclusion: S2Dialog通过统一的框架有效解决了对话级多模态检索中语义与风格联合建模的难题，验证了对话级文本-声学对比学习的有效性，为对话相关下游任务提供了可靠的检索基础。

Abstract: Multimodal dialogue retrieval aims to retrieve dialogues from multimodal dialogue banks that are similar to a target dialogue in terms of both textual semantics and acoustic conversational styles. Such dialogue-level retrieval is crucial for many dialogue-related tasks, including Emotion Recognition in Conversation, Spoken Dialogue Systems, and Conversational Speech Synthesis, where external dialogue examples can provide valuable semantic and stylistic references. However, existing retrieval methods are still largely limited to utterance-level or unimodal matching, and often fail to capture the global semantic coherence and stylistic consistency of an entire dialogue. To address this gap, we propose S2Dialog, a unified framework for dialogue-level semantic-style retrieval from multimodal dialogue banks. Specifically, S2Dialog consists of a Dialogue-level Textual Retriever and a Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever, which encode the textual and acoustic modalities of a dialogue into dialogue-level representations, respectively. To further enhance multimodal retrieval, we introduce Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning, which aligns semantically and stylistically similar dialogues while distinguishing unrelated ones. Extensive experiments on the multimodal dialogue dataset DailyTalk demonstrate that S2Dialog achieves outstanding retrieval performance.

</details>


### [89] [HERMES: a multi-agent framework for structured knowledge extraction from ultra-long documents in geoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14055)
*Ziqi Song,Zongyuan Xiang,James G. Ogg,Bruce S. Lieberman,Gabi Ogg,Natalia López Carranza,Wen Du,Yufei Ye,Shuan Li,Zhong Peng,Shaoqi Yu,Juye Wei,Ying Zhou,Jieping Ye,Jiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍HERMES，一个可扩展的多智能体框架，用于从超长科学文献中提取结构化数据。该系统应用于55卷《无脊椎古生物学专论》，构建了包含32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库，实体和属性的平均F1分数分别约为0.90和0.91，每卷提取效率比纯人工基线提高约六倍，且无需额外训练即可迁移到古地磁学和地球化学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球科学领域的权威科学知识仍大量封存于传统专著和历史文献中，其非结构化文本和复杂版面阻碍了计算访问，需要一种可扩展的方法将这些历史文献转化为机器可读的结构化数据。

Method: HERMES采用协调型大语言模型构建多智能体框架，将领域约束、验证规则和证据追踪整合到统一的文档级提取流程中，同时解析文本、表格、图形和标题等多元内容。

Result: 在55卷《无脊椎古生物学专论》上生成含32,277个分类实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库（在线发布于treatise.geolex.org），跨化石类群提取性能稳定（实体平均F1约0.90，属性平均F1约0.91），每卷效率较纯人工基线提升约六倍；在古地磁学和地球化学领域的评估无需额外模型训练即展示出跨领域迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条将历史科学文献转化为FAIR导向结构化数据的实用路径，为数据密集型学科和大规模知识整合提供了可持续基础设施。

Abstract: Authoritative scientific knowledge in geoscience remains largely trapped in legacy monographs and historical literature, where unstructured text and complex layouts hinder computational access. We introduce HERMES, a scalable multi-agent framework that extracts structured data from ultra-long scientific documents. Using a coordinating large language model, HERMES integrates domain constraints, validation rules and evidence tracing within a unified document-level extraction process that incorporates parsed text, tables, figures and captions. Applied to the 55-volume Treatise on Invertebrate Paleontology, the system produced a structured database of 32,277 fossil taxonomic entities and 451,878 attributes, released online at https://treatise.geolex.org. Extraction performance remained stable across fossil groups (average F1 scores of approximately 0.90 for entities and 0.91 for attributes), improving per-volume efficiency approximately sixfold relative to the tested fully manual baseline. Evaluation in palaeomagnetism and geochemistry, conducted without additional model training, demonstrated transfer across distinct geoscience domains. This work provides a practical pathway to transform historical scientific literature into FAIR-oriented structured data, offering a sustainable infrastructure for data-intensive disciplines and large-scale knowledge integration.

</details>


### [90] [The conditional superiority of fast silicon sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14079)
*Nickolas Hock Yuen Lam,Ji Xuan Voo,Xiangyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了"快速"与"慢速"硅基采样（silicon sampling）在算法保真度上的差异，基于新加坡全国代表性调查数据。研究发现硅基采样仍处于早期发展阶段，虽能中等程度准确估计总体均值，但会低估意见方差并扭曲潜在语境空间。在现有局限下，"快速"硅基采样在计算效率和运行时间上显著优于"慢速"模式，且在算法保真度上呈单调优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索硅基采样（利用大语言模型模拟人类调查受访者）在加速执行时是否会损失其估计总体人口参数的保真度。这是对快速硅基采样这一新兴方法论的系统性验证与扩展。

Method: 以新加坡全国代表性调查受访者为对象，系统比较"快速"与"慢速"两种硅基采样模式的算法保真度，评估其对总体均值、意见方差及潜在语境空间的估计表现，并比较两者的计算资源与运行效率。

Result: 硅基采样尚不能完全替代传统调查方法：其对总体均值的估计中等保真，但对意见方差存在系统性低估，且对潜在语境空间的表征存在扭曲。然而，"快速"硅基采样在算法保真度上单调优于"慢速"模式，同时在计算效率和运行时间上显著更优。

Conclusion: 硅基采样（即使采用前沿模型）仍处于早期发展阶段，仅应在极度谨慎的前提下使用。但在既定限制条件下，更快的采样策略反而更具优势，这挑战了"慢速即更高质量"的直觉假设，为该领域的后续优化提供了方向性指引。

Abstract: Silicon sampling can produce surprisingly good population estimates at times. Does doing it fast attenuate such fidelity? In this study, we extend and assess ongoing work in silicon sampling by comparing the algorithmic fidelity of "fast" and "slow" modes of silicon sampling among a nationally representative sample of Singaporean survey respondents. We find that silicon sampling with contemporary frontier models remains a method in early development to be used only with great caution. While silicon samples are able to produce moderately faithful estimates of population means, they continue to understate opinion variance and distort the latent contextual space behind human opinions. Conditional on such limitations, we find "fast" modes of silicon sampling to be relatively superior to traditional "slow" modes of silicon sampling. Fast silicon sampling is significantly more efficient in compute resources and run-time while being monotonically superior to slower modes of sampling in algorithmic fidelity.

</details>


### [91] [Leading-Silence Augmentation and Multi-Stage Synthetic Supervision for the Second MLC-SLM Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14150)
*Kexin Shi,Renhe Sun,Yuge Huang,Ximeng Wang,Jiayi Zhou,Jian Liu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了第二届多语言对话语音语言模型挑战赛(MLC-SLM)中针对两个任务的解决方案：任务1为说话人分离与识别，任务2为对话语音理解。任务1通过随机前导静音裁剪、时间戳修正和指数移动平均训练策略微调VibeVoice-ASR-7B模型；任务2通过合成问答对数据增强和带标签直接回答微调Qwen3-Omni-30B模型。实验表明裁剪和EMA分别将tcpMER从18.30%降至17.27%和16.73%，数据增强与直接回答策略将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLC-SLM挑战赛要求在不提供语料边界和说话人标签的完整非分段多语言对话上同时完成说话人分离/识别和对话语音理解两项任务，评估时需端到端处理真实对话场景，且任务2不提供问答训练集，给模型训练与推理带来挑战。

Method: 任务1使用VibeVoice-ASR-7B微调，采用随机前导静音裁剪以增强对静音段的鲁棒性、一致的时间戳修正以及EMA训练策略。任务2使用Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct，通过多模态候选生成、静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强构建合成问答对，并采用带标签直接回答方式进行微调。

Result: 任务1：裁剪使tcpMER从18.30%降至17.27%，EMA进一步降至16.73%；任务2：联合分布匹配增强和带标签直接回答将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据处理与训练策略在两项任务上均取得了有效提升，证明了随机静音裁剪、EMA训练以及合成问答数据增强方法在无边界标注的多语言对话语音任务中的实用性。

Abstract: The second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge evaluates two tasks over complete, unsegmented multilingual conversations: speaker diarization and recognition (Task 1) and conversational speech understanding (Task 2). Neither task provides oracle utterance boundaries or speaker labels at evaluation, and Task 2 provides no question-answer training set. For Task 1, we fine-tune VibeVoice-ASR-7B with random leading-silence cropping, consistent timestamp correction, and an exponential moving average (EMA) training strategy. For Task 2, we construct synthetic question-answer pairs through multimodal candidate generation, silent-audio filtering, and distribution-matched augmentation, and fine-tune Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct for tagged direct answering. On the Task 1 evaluation set, cropping reduces tcpMER from 18.30% to 17.27%, and EMA further reduces it to 16.73%. On the Task 2 evaluation set, jointly applying distribution-matched augmentation and tagged direct answering raises accuracy from 83.0% to 86.0%.

</details>


### [92] [How Much Do Legal RAG Systems Still Hallucinate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14210)
*Souvick Das,Sallam Abualhaija,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对法律领域八个RAG系统在两个法律语料库（英语GDPR和法语国家民法典）上的幻觉行为进行了细粒度分析，采用声明级和答案级评估，发现幻觉问题依然普遍存在，最佳系统的幻觉率低于10%，而最差系统接近50%，且包含错误前提的问题会导致更高的幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域的RAG系统中幻觉问题可能导致严重后果，但此前缺乏对该领域幻觉行为的细粒度理解，需要系统性分析来认识其严重程度和模式。

Method: 对八个法律RAG系统在两个法律语料库（英语GDPR和法语国家民法典）上进行评估，采用声明级（claim-level）和答案级（answer-level）两种评估方式，从幻觉密度（hallucination density）和严重程度（severity）两个维度分析，并按问题类别和用户画像进行分类评估，同时使用142个法律专家撰写的独立问题集进行验证。

Result: 幻觉在法律RAG系统中仍然普遍存在，最佳系统的幻觉响应率低于10%，而最差系统接近一半的响应包含幻觉。此外，含有错误前提（需被拒绝的虚假假设）的问题在法律专家撰写的问题集上产生了较高的幻觉率。

Conclusion: 法律领域的RAG系统幻觉问题远未解决，尤其在处理包含错误前提的问题时表现不佳，这提示需要更有效的幻觉缓解策略，并关注对虚假前提的识别与拒绝能力。

Abstract: Hallucination is a major challenge for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in the legal domain, where ungrounded answers can lead to serious consequences. To better understand this problem, we conduct a fine-grained analysis of hallucination behavior in eight legal RAG systems across two legal corpora, the GDPR (in English) and a national civil law (in French). Using claim-level and answer-level evaluation, we report on hallucination density and severity, analyze performance across question categories and user personas, and validate our findings on an independent set of 142 legal-expert-authored questions. Our results show that hallucinations remain pervasive, ranging from less than 10% of responses for the best-performing systems to nearly half in the worst case. We further find that false-premise questions, containing incorrect assumptions that must be rejected, produce high hallucination rates on the manually-drafted questions.

</details>


### [93] [The More Popular, The Harder to Forget: Adaptive Popularity for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14229)
*Anna Borisiuk,Andrey Savchenko,Alexander Panchenko,Elena Tutubalina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AdaPop（自适应流行度）方法，利用本地token置信度与基于外部流行度代理（如Wikidata链接数、LLM-as-Judge）的逐事实指数，结合双上升控制器自动调节遗忘-保留平衡，在三个模型族和两个基准上，相较现有方法在改写查询下泄露减少约5倍，对抗性改写下减少约1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法对所有事实施加统一的梯度压力，忽略了训练数据频率的影响——高频事实在预训练中记忆更深、更难消除，而低频事实则相反。这种均匀处理的策略导致遗忘效果不佳或保留能力受损。

Method: AdaPop结合两个核心组件：(1) 将本地token置信度与逐事实流行度相关的指数相乘作为遗忘压力，流行度由外部代理（如Wikidata sitelinks、LLM-as-Judge）估计；(2) 通过双上升控制器每epoch自动调整保留惩罚项，实现遗忘与保留的自动平衡。

Result: 在三个模型族和两个基准上的实验表明，AdaPop在改写查询下的遗忘内容泄露比竞争方法少约5倍，在对抗性改写下少约1.6倍。内部指标进一步验证：AdaPop下遗忘集的隐藏状态较预遗忘模型的偏移更大，而保留集表示保持接近。

Conclusion: 考虑训练数据频率的差异化遗忘压力能显著提升LLM遗忘效果；通过外部代理估计流行度并结合自动平衡控制器，可以在增强遗忘的同时有效保护保留知识。

Abstract: Popular facts are memorised more deeply during pretraining and resist removal longer than rare ones, yet existing LLM unlearning methods apply uniform gradient pressure regardless of training-data frequency. We propose the AdaPop (Adaptive Popularity) method, which combines local token confidence with a per-fact popularity-dependent exponent derived from an external proxy (e.g., Wikidata sitelinks, LLM-as-Judge), and automates the forget-retain balance via a dual-ascent controller that adjusts the retain penalty each epoch. Across three model families and two benchmarks, AdaPop leaks ~5x less forgotten content than competing methods under paraphrased queries and ~1.6x less under adversarial reformulations. We support our analysis with internal metrics: under our method, forget-set hidden states move further from the pre-unlearning model's states than under other methods, while retain-set representations remain close.

</details>


### [94] [SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14277)
*Haonan He,Haodi Lei,Yun Luo,Haoran Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yizhuo Li,Shengji Tang,Zhilin Wang,Runzhe Zhan,Lei Bai,Ganqu Cui,Fangchen Yu,Yafu Li,Peng Ye,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究从长上下文推理教师模型（SU-01）向短上下文学生模型进行on-policy蒸馏（OPD）以迁移证明推理能力。为应对tokenizer不匹配，在共享文本空间中进行OPD并仅对齐文本跨度一致的token；为缓解生成长度爆炸和频繁截断，引入学生参考KL损失并掩码终止token的优势。实验在Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4等多个学生模型上取得一致提升，Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分达到55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro，并在科学基准上也表现出跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索从长上下文推理能力的强教师模型（如SU-01）向短上下文学生模型蒸馏推理能力的可行性，并解决该设置下特有的实际挑战：tokenizer不匹配、师生分布不匹配、响应长度爆炸和训练不稳定等问题。

Method: 1) 在共享文本空间中执行OPD，仅对齐学生和教师tokenizer下占用相同文本跨度的token，解决tokenizer差异；2) 引入学生参考KL损失，约束学生不过度偏离初始策略，缓解分布不匹配；3) 掩码特殊终止token（如</think>和<|im_end|>）的优势，抑制生成长度爆炸。

Result: 在多种学生模型（同族和异族，包括Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4）上均取得数学推理能力的一致提升，尤其在自然语言数学证明上效果显著。Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分至55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro；同时提升HLE、HiPhO等科学基准，表明OPD迁移的推理能力可泛化到数学训练域之外。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了一种实用的长上下文教师向短上下文学生迁移推理能力的OPD方案，通过文本空间token对齐、参考KL约束和终止token掩码等策略，有效克服了多方面的训练挑战，并在多个模型家族和跨域基准上验证了方法的鲁棒性和泛化性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a promising way to transfer reasoning capabilities from stronger teacher models, but applying it to long-context reasoning teachers and short-context students introduces practical challenges, including tokenizer mismatch, teacher-student distribution mismatch, response length explosion, and training instability. In this work, we study this setting by transferring proof-reasoning capabilities from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 to short-context student models. To handle tokenizer differences, we perform OPD in a shared text space and align only tokens that occupy identical text spans under the student and teacher tokenizers. To mitigate the problem of excessive generation length and frequent truncation, we introduce a student reference KL loss and mask the advantages of special termination tokens such as </think> and <|im_end|>. This strategy constrains the student from drifting excessively from its initial policy, thereby mitigating the teacher-student distribution mismatch problem and fostering steady length growth. Experiments on both same-family and different-family student models, including Qwen3, Qwen3.5, Intern-S2, GLM-4.7, Gemma-4, show consistent gains in mathematical reasoning, especially natural-language math proving. Notably, Intern-S2-Preview improves by 21.2 points on ProofBench, reaching 55.2 and surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro. It also improves on science benchmarks such as HLE and HiPhO, suggesting that OPD transfers reasoning capabilities that generalize beyond the mathematical training domain.

</details>


### [95] [Envs-FORGE: Frontier-Optimized Reward-Grounded Environment Synthesis for Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14312)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Zhichao Shi,Hao Zhou,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Envs-FORGE，一种将验证器奖励转化为逐个种子环境合成动作的提示策略。它通过估计种子通过率、评估围绕目标学习前沿的六个投影-方向动作，并求解逐种子混合整数线性规划（MILP）来选择生成条件。在Qwen 3.5 35B上，相比Base，tb-core Pass@1提升9.2个百分点，tb-2.0提升6.4个百分点，超过最强固定配方基线2.4和2.1个百分点，SWE-bench Verified达77.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端代理的强化学习训练依赖可执行的训练环境，且需要可靠的奖励与合适的难度。固定配方（如few-shot、Self-Instruct、Evol-Instruct）对每个种子应用相同的提示策略，无法根据当前策略的实际需求动态调整任务难度或方向，导致训练效率受限。

Method: Envs-FORGE的核心流程包括：(1) 估计每个种子的通过率以定位当前能力边界；(2) 评估围绕目标学习前沿的六个投影-方向动作（如更难/更易/不同任务等）；(3) 求解逐种子混合整数线性规划（MILP）选择最优动作以调节生成条件；(4) 所选动作驱动指令、夹具、oracle解决方案、测试和Docker环境的同步重写，仅将通过黄金验证的包进入RL训练。此外，索引形式的MILP支持可选的软技能覆盖用于组合规划。

Result: 在Qwen 3.5 35B上，Envs-FORGE相比Base将tb-core的Pass@1从40.0%提升至49.2%（+9.2个百分点），tb-2.0从23.0%提升至29.4%（+6.4个百分点），分别超过最强固定配方基线2.4和2.1个百分点。在SWE-bench Verified上达到77.1%（Base为73.4%）。在4B-35B多个模型规模上，tb-core提升6.8-9.2个百分点。所有方法均产出100个验证环境，使用2.27M-2.88M合成token，保证了下游训练集规模与运行规模的一致性。

Conclusion: Envs-FORGE证明了将验证器奖励动态转换为逐种子环境合成动作的有效性，相比固定配方策略在多个基准和模型规模上均取得一致提升，且不增加额外训练集规模或运行开销。代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for terminal agents needs executable training environments with reliable rewards and useful difficulty. Fixed recipes such as few-shot, Self-Instruct, and Evol-Instruct apply the same prompting policy to every seed, even when the current policy would benefit from a harder, easier, or simply different task. We present Envs-FORGE, a prompting policy that converts verifier rewards into per-seed environment-synthesis actions. Envs-FORGE estimates seed pass rates, scores six projection--direction actions around a target learning frontier, and solves a per-seed mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to choose the action that conditions generation. The selected action drives synchronized rewriting of the instruction, fixtures, oracle solution, tests, and Docker environment; only gold-verified bundles enter RL training. The indexed MILP form also supports optional soft skill coverage for portfolio planning. On Qwen 3.5 35B, Envs-FORGE improves Pass@1 over Base by 9.2 percentage points on tb-core (40.0% to 49.2%) and 6.4 points on tb-2.0 (23.0% to 29.4%), exceeding the strongest fixed-recipe baseline by 2.4 and 2.1 points. It reaches 77.1% on SWE-bench Verified versus 73.4% for Base, and improves tb-core by 6.8--9.2 points across the evaluated 4B--35B models. All synthesis methods export 100 verified environments and use 2.27M--2.88M synthesis tokens, placing the comparison at the same downstream training-set size and the same operational scale. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/DataArc-SynData-Toolkit/.

</details>


### [96] [Local and Global Regimes of Geometric Complexity in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14361)
*Arwa Osman,Marco Baroni,Iuri Macocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究词汇多样性（数据集中唯一最后词元项的数量）如何影响语言模型表示的内在维度（ID）估计。发现存在尺度依赖的双重机制转换：低词汇多样性时词汇少导致更高ID，高词汇多样性时反之。作者推导出一个精确无参数的逆转点公式，与所有实验尺度下的观测转换点吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在维度（ID）常被用于探测语言模型的表征复杂度，但ID差异究竟反映语言本身的属性，还是数据集构建方式的伪影，此前尚不清楚。本文旨在厘清词汇多样性这一具体的数据集属性对ID估计的影响，以避免对ID结果的误解。

Method: 聚焦于词汇多样性（数据集中唯一最后词元项的数量）对ID估计的影响，通过系统改变词汇多样性观察ID的变化规律，发现存在两种ID机制（低词汇多样性机制与高词汇多样性机制），并推导出一个精确的、无参数的公式来刻画逆转发生的临界点。

Result: 发现尺度依赖的转换现象：低词汇多样性条件下，唯一最终词较少的条件产生更高的ID；而在高词汇多样性条件下，顺序反转，唯一词更多的条件产生更高的ID。推导出的精确无参数逆转点公式在所有实验尺度下都与观测到的转换点一致。

Conclusion: 一方面，研究结果警示在将一组表征的内在维度解释为复杂度直接线索时需要谨慎；另一方面，两种ID机制的发现揭示了LLM中语言数据组织的一般原理，为理解其内部流形结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Intrinsic dimensionality (ID) is widely used to probe the representational complexity of language models, but it remains unclear whether ID differences reflect properties of language itself or artefacts of how the underlying dataset was constructed. In this paper, we focus specifically on how lexical diversity, the number of unique last-token items present in a dataset, affects ID estimates of that dataset. We find a scale-dependent transition between two regimes: at low lexical diversity, conditions with fewer unique final words produce higher ID, while at high lexical diversity, this ordering reverses, and conditions with more unique words produce higher ID. We derive an exact, parameter-free formula for the point at which this reversal occurs, which matches the observed transition point at every scale tested. On the one hand, our results highlight how care must be taken when interpreting the intrinsic dimensionality of a set of representations as a straightforward cue of their complexity. On the other hand, our discovery of the two ID regimes reveals a general principle of organisation of linguistic data in LLMs that sheds new light on their inner manifold structures.

</details>


### [97] [A Survey of Large Models in Sports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14377)
*Yichen Xu,Jianzhe Ma,Chuhan Wang,Zhonghao Cao,Liangyu Chen,Wenxuan Wang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了大模型（尤其是多模态大语言模型）在体育领域的研究现状，涵盖任务与应用、数据集与基准，以及挑战与未来方向，并提供了开源的GitHub资源库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育在全球范围内日益重要，而（多模态）大语言模型的快速发展为其在体育理解、分析和交互方面带来了变革潜力。目前缺乏对这一快速发展的交叉领域进行全面系统的梳理，需要建立研究基础以推动该方向的发展。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，从三个维度展开：(i) 按不同参与者群体梳理大模型在体育中的任务与应用；(ii) 详细分析体育相关的数据集与基准测试；(iii) 批判性讨论当前面临的挑战与未来研究方向。

Result: 呈现了对大模型在体育领域应用的全面综述，包括现有任务分类、数据集概览以及关键挑战的识别，并维护了一个开源GitHub仓库作为持续更新的资源中心。

Conclusion: 本文旨在为大模型驱动的体育智能研究和实际应用奠定基础，为后续研究者提供系统的参考框架和资源指引。

Abstract: Sports have witnessed growing global enthusiasm in recent years, serving as a vital force for physical health, cultural exchange, social connection, and economic growth. The rapid advancement of large models, particularly (multimodal) large language models (M)LLMs, has demonstrated transformative potential to reshape sports understanding, analysis, and interaction across diverse domains. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of large models in sports, including (i) an overview of tasks and applications across different participant groups; (ii) a detailed analysis of sports-related datasets and benchmarks; and (iii) a critical discussion of current challenges and future directions. Our goal is to establish a foundation for advancing research and practical development of large-model-driven sports intelligence. An open-source GitHub repository is maintained at: https://github.com/Road2Redemption/Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports1.

</details>


### [98] [Information Satisfaction: A Reader-Centered Axis for Summarization Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14457)
*Isabel Cachola,William Walden,Reno Kriz,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有摘要评估指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore及LLM-as-judge）只关注通用摘要质量或特定属性，无法衡量摘要在个性化读者背景下的信息效用。作者通过扰动测试和专家人工评估发现，即使强LLM评估指标也无法通过基本信息内容扰动测试，且传统与基于LLM的指标在衡量信息满意度方面与人类判断的一致性很差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际用户（如生物医学研究者与家庭医生）有不同的信息需求，但查询聚焦式摘要无法充分捕捉这种差异。读者的背景或人物角色（persona）在不同查询间相对稳定，可作为衡量摘要是否满足读者需求的实际信号。然而现有指标并未考虑这一点，因此需要探究现有摘要评估指标对信息性和人物角色差异的敏感程度。

Method: 对流行摘要评估指标进行信息内容和人物角色的敏感性测试（包含基本信息内容扰动测试），并进行专家人工评估实验，基于特定人物背景和使用场景衡量信息满意度，与自动指标结果对比。

Result: 许多流行指标（包括强LLM-as-judge指标）无法通过基本信息内容扰动测试；传统指标和LLM基指标在衡量信息满意度方面均不足，与人类判断的一致性较差。

Conclusion: 现有摘要评估指标不足以衡量个性化读者的信息满意度，需要开发考虑读者人物角色与信息需求的新评估方法。

Abstract: The majority of work on summarization evaluation focuses on general summary quality (e.g., ROUGE, BERTScore) or specific desired properties (e.g., readability, factuality). However, these metrics fail to measure the utility of a summary to an individual user. For example, a biomedical researcher learning about the latest vaccine research will have different informational needs from a family doctor. Query-focused summarization captures part of this need, but in practice, users rarely state everything relevant in a query: a single short query is likely inadequate to distinguish the needs of a researcher from those of a physician. By contrast, a reader's background or persona (their role and expertise) is comparatively stable across queries and recovers much of this missing context, which makes it a practical signal for assessing whether a summary satisfies that reader's needs. In this work, we assess how sensitive popular summarization metrics are to both informational and persona differences, and find that many popular metrics, including strong LLM-as-judge metrics, fail basic perturbation tests of informational content. We additionally conduct an expert human evaluation, measuring summary preferences based on information satisfaction given a specific person's background and use case. We find that both traditional and LLM-based metrics are insufficient measures of information satisfaction and agree poorly with human judgment.

</details>


### [99] [You Only Pass Once: Answering and Abstaining Together in a Single Forward Pass of a Frozen Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14465)
*Ziyang Luo,Zhongyao Chu,Xinjie He,Youting Wang,Xukui Qin,Runxiong Wu,Yan-Syuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出YOPO系统，通过训练一个小型网络重构被引导（steering）操作改写前的残差流，使冻结语言模型在一次前向传播中同时完成回答、推理引导和"答案-弃权"（answer-or-abstain）判断，解决了引导写入与充分性检测方向读取之间的干扰问题，在多项基准上超越两次前向传播的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型在推理任务上有两个耦合缺陷：一是未充分利用自身残差流中已有的证据，二是无法检测输入信息是否充分，导致产生虚构回答。已有两方向分别解决此问题：条件引导探针和零样本充分性方向，但在同一残差流上部署时会互相干扰。

Method: 保持充分性检测方向（direction）不变，训练一个小型网络重构（以steered与clean残差对的均方误差为损失，无需充分性标签）被引导改写前的残差，然后在重构结果上读取检测方向。构建YOPO（You Only Pass Once）系统，在冻结Qwen2.5主干（1.5B/3B/7B）上一次前向传播完成回答、引导和弃权。

Result: 三路准确率超过冻结基线两倍以上（1.5B alphaNLI上0.375→0.798）；单次前向传播在所有规模上超越两次前向传播基线（0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863），并在六个模型家族十个背骨上成立；提出首个月answer-or-abstain基准，其门控在域内数据集上全部最优，无标签方向是唯一能跨域迁移的门族。

Conclusion: YOPO证明可以通过残差重构消除引导与弃权检测在同一前向传播中的干扰，实现"一次前向传播"完成回答、引导与弃权，同时验证了"弃权不应被训练进来"的容量转移原则，并在原生标签复制（SQuAD2、RepLiQA、MuSiQue）上锚定架构主张，为answer-or-abstain提供了首个标准四域套件基准。

Abstract: A frozen language model on reasoning tasks has two coupled weaknesses: it under-uses evidence its own residual stream already encodes, and it fails to detect when the input is insufficient to answer, so it confabulates. This paper consolidates two research lines that address these on the same residual stream: a conditional steering probe writes the stream at mid-stack layers and recovers reasoning accuracy from a frozen backbone, and a zero-shot sufficiency direction reads the stream and abstains when information is insufficient. Deployed in one forward pass they interfere: the steering write shifts the state the direction reads, costing up to 8 AUROC points of cross-domain transfer on small models; a separate clean pass doubles inference cost. We keep the direction fixed and train a small network to reconstruct the pre-steering residual from the steered one -- mean-squared error on (steered, clean) pairs, no sufficiency labels -- and read the direction on the reconstruction. The resulting system, YOPO (You Only Pass Once), answers, steers, and abstains in one forward pass of a frozen Qwen2.5 backbone (1.5B/3B/7B). End to end, three-way accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline (0.375->0.798 on 1.5B alphaNLI) and one pass beats the two-pass reference at every scale (0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863) and on ten backbones across six model families. We chart the capacity-transfer frontier quantifying the principle that abstention should not be trained in; a source-side audit catches our own alphaNLI construction leaking a surface artifact, so architectural claims are anchored on native-label replications (SQuAD2, RepLiQA, MuSiQue); and on the standard four-domain suite we contribute, to our knowledge, the first answer-or-abstain benchmark, where our gate tops every in-domain dataset and the label-free direction is the only gate family to survive domain transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [SN-ASMO: Satellite-Navigation Array Spatial-Manifold Precise Observation Theory A Mathematical Foundation for Observation Formation, Unified U(1) Geometry, Intrinsic Information, and Preservation of the RTK Integer Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13611)
*Xianwei Meng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出SN-ASMO框架，统一处理压制干扰、高动态运动和载波相位RTK，在数学封闭且物理可检验的框架内实现观测形成、参考协方差、精确干扰商、同源传输、内在统计信息与RTK整数结构保持的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当接收机通过阵列几何、信道状态、权重和信号处理规则主动参与观测形成时，载波观测如何继续代表同一客观传播过程并保持连续相位和整数模糊度结构，是一个根本性问题。缺乏传播诱导与观测者诱导变化的严格分离，会使抗干扰、高动态和精密载波相位定位在观测形成层面结构耦合。

Method: 提出SN-ASMO（Suppression-interference Navigation with Active-array Signal-transformation and Metric-invariant Observation）框架，从观测物理学角度将观测形成过程建模为可复现、可证伪的协议，具体涵盖静态干扰、动态旋转、分支间定时失配、近零响应、RF重放和RTK整数风险验证等场景。

Result: 该框架在数学上封闭且物理上可检验，能够在观测形成层面实现观测形成、参考协方差、精确干扰商、同源传输、内在统计信息以及RTK整数结构的统一保持。

Conclusion: SN-ASMO的核心创新在于将观测形成、参考协方差、精确干扰商、同源传输、内在统计信息与RTK整数结构保持统一到一个数学封闭且物理可测试的框架中，为解决抗干扰与精密定位在观测层面的结构耦合提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: Suppressive interference, high-dynamic motion of the receiving platform, and carrier-phase RTK lead satellite navigation to one fundamental question: when the receiver actively participates in observation formation through its array geometry, channel states, weights, and signal-processing rules, how can the resulting carrier observation continue to represent the same objective propagation process while preserving continuous phase and the integer-ambiguity structure? The physical world is not changed by a reconfiguration of the observer, but its complex representation in the observation world is. Without a strict separation between propagation-induced and observer-induced variations, anti-jamming, high dynamics, and precise carrier-phase positioning become structurally coupled at the observation-formation level.
  Finally, reproducible and falsifiable protocols are provided for static interference, dynamic rotation, inter-branch timing mismatch, near-null response, RF replay, and RTK integer-risk validation. The scoped novelty of SN-ASMO is the unification of observation formation, reference covariance, exact nuisance quotients, same-source transport, intrinsic statistical information, and preservation of the RTK integer structure in one mathematically closed and physically testable framework.

</details>


### [101] [A Survey of Typical-Cell Volume Distributions in Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay Tessellations: Analytical Theory, High-Dimensional Limits, and Wireless Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13615)
*Minghua Xia,Tian Shi,Wenkunn Wen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述了由泊松点过程生成的泊松-沃罗诺伊（Poisson–Voronoi）与泊松-德劳内（Poisson–Delaunay）三角剖分中典型胞元体积分布的研究进展，对比了两者在分析上的不对称性，并讨论了无线网络中的应用及开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管泊松-沃罗诺伊与泊松-德劳内剖分是随机几何、计算几何和无线网络分析中的经典对偶模型，但文献中关于其典型胞元体积分布的分析结果存在显著不对称性：德劳内单纯形体积具有维度显式的PDF、CDF和矩公式，而沃罗诺伊胞元体积虽已有部分精确表示，但缺乏无条件的闭式分布。作者旨在通过系统综述弥合这一差距，激励进一步研究。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了泊松-沃罗诺伊和泊松-德劳内剖分典型胞元体积分布的主要分析方法与已知结果，包括：平面上沃罗诺伊胞元体积的精确积分表示、一般维度的尺度-形状分解与条件Gamma结构、德劳内单纯形基于Mellin变换和Meijer G函数表示的显式结果，以及高维极限行为，并结合无线网络应用场景进行归纳。

Result: 文章总结了沃罗诺伊胞元与德劳内单纯形体积分布在解析可处理性上的不对称性：德劳内情形已有完整的维度显式闭式解，而沃罗诺伊情形仅有条件结构或近似结果，实际建模主要依赖仿真和矩刻画。同时归纳了高维极限规律及无线网络中的负载建模、协作传输和三维架构等应用。

Conclusion: 论文指出现有研究仍有若干关键开放问题，包括：无条件泊松-沃罗诺伊分布的推导、非泊松空间模型的扩展、数据驱动的几何推断以及维度感知的网络建模。这些方向被视为未来研究的重点。

Abstract: Random spatial tessellations generated by point processes provide fundamental models for proximity, space partitioning, and local geometry in stochastic systems. Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay tessellations induced by homogeneous Poisson point processes form a canonical dual pair used in stochastic geometry, computational geometry, spatial statistics, and wireless-network analysis. Their typical-cell volume distributions provide important geometric inputs for modeling coverage, traffic load, clustering, connectivity, and other system characteristics. Despite extensive study, the literature remains analytically asymmetric. For Poisson--Voronoi cell volumes, exact integral representations exist in certain planar settings, while a recent scale--shape factorization provides an exact general-dimensional representation with conditional Gamma structure. However, the normalized shape laws and unbounded facet-count mixture remain implicit, and tractable unconditional closed-form distributions are unavailable. Practical modeling therefore relies largely on simulation, moment characterizations, and empirical approximations. By contrast, Poisson--Delaunay simplex volumes admit dimension-explicit PDFs, CDFs, and moment formulas derived through Mellin-transform analysis and Meijer's \(G\)-function representations. Motivated by this contrast, this paper surveys typical-cell volume distributions in Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay tessellations. We review the main analytical methods, synthesize exact and approximate results, summarize emerging high-dimensional limits, and discuss wireless-network applications, including load modeling, cooperative transmission, and three-dimensional architectures. We also identify open problems concerning unconditional Poisson--Voronoi distributions, non-Poisson spatial models, data-driven geometric inference, and dimension-aware network modeling.

</details>


### [102] [Energy-Aware Compression-Computation Co-Adaptation for Latency Minimization in Multi-User Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13632)
*Loc X. Nguyen,Yumin Park,Avi Deb Raha,Huy Q. Le,Zhu Han,Eui-Nam Huh,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能量感知的压缩-计算协同自适应（CoCo）框架，用于DeepJSCC语义通信系统，以应对用户设备在信道质量、QoS目标和本地能量方面的异构性。通过联合优化参数（鲁棒编解码器）和资源分配（贪心子载波分配），CoCo在不需要重新训练的情况下适应多样化的压缩率和信道噪声，相比仅速率自适应或仅去噪的方法降低了总延迟并满足每个用户的个性化需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DeepJSCC语义通信方案难以适应不同用户在信道质量、预期QoS目标和可用本地能量上的异构性差异，缺乏一个能够统一考虑这些因素并做出最优折衷的系统性方案。

Method: 首先将用户异构性（QoS、信道条件、本地能量）显式建模为数学优化问题，提出CoCo框架（基站与本地设备间进行信号传输长度与任务卸载的协同决策）。将原问题分解为两个子问题：参数优化（设计无需重新训练即可适应多种压缩率和信道噪声的鲁棒编解码器）和资源分配（采用贪心子载波分配以降低通信时间）。

Result: 在标准图像数据集和加性高斯白噪声信道上的仿真结果表明，CoCo相比仅速率自适应的DeepJSCC或仅去噪的方法，能够降低总延迟，并确保每个独立用户的QoS需求得到满足。

Conclusion: CoCo通过能量感知的压缩-计算协同自适应，有效解决了语义通信中用户异构性问题，在保证各用户QoS的前提下优化了系统资源利用率。

Abstract: Deep joint source-channel coding-enabled (DeepJSCC) semantic communication (SemCom) has excelled at delivering high perceptual quality at low channel-bandwidth ratios, which positions it as a pillar for next-generation wireless networks. However, the existing works have difficulty accommodating user heterogeneity in terms of communication channel quality, expected quality-of-service (QoS) targets, and the available local energy. Therefore, in this paper, we explicitly reflect the heterogeneity of user devices in terms of the differences in expected QoS, channel condition, and local energy, and then mathematically formulate the problem. Next, we propose an energy-aware compression-computation co-adaptation (CoCo) framework, in which the base station can meet the expected user QoS by transmitting a longer signal or offloading the task to a local device. The user has to dedicate energy to denoising the signal to recover higher-fidelity latent features before feeding it to the semantic decoder. To solve the formulated problem, we first decompose it into two sub-problems: parameter optimization and resource allocation problems. Specifically, we propose a robust codec that effectively works under a diversity of compression rates and channel noise without re-training, while the greedy sub-carrier allocation lowers the communication time. Finally, we present simulation results on standard image datasets over additive white Gaussian noise to demonstrate the effectiveness of CoCo, which reduces total latency relative to rate-only adaptive DeepJSCC or denoising-only, thereby ensuring the demands of each individual user are met.

</details>


### [103] [Optimal Power Allocation and AI Receiver Design for Superimposed DMRS and Data Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13809)
*Sha Hu,Zhongwang Fu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对基于OFDM的MIMO系统，研究了叠加DMRS与数据（SI-DMRS）的传输方案。首先推导了迭代信道估计与检测（ICED）过程中信道估计均方误差（MSE）与MIMO检测MSE之间迭代行为的解析框架，并利用该框架优化DMRS与数据符号之间的功率分配和导频图案。其次，设计了一种基于Transformer编码器、嵌入ICED结构的人工智能（AI）接收机。仿真结果表明，所提AI-ICED接收机结合SI-DMRS相比传统非重叠DMRS和数据符号系统可有效提升频谱效率（SE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OFDM MIMO系统中DMRS与数据符号在时频资源上非重叠，导致导频开销占用宝贵的频谱资源，限制频谱效率。为提高SE，需要探索DMRS与数据叠加传输的方案，并解决其带来的信道估计与检测相互耦合的挑战，同时需要有效的接收机设计来支撑该传输机制。

Method: 1）建立解析框架刻画ICED过程中CE与MD的MSE迭代行为；2）基于该框架对SI-DMRS传输的功率分配和导频图案进行优化；3）设计基于Transformer编码器的AI接收机，其中嵌入ICED结构以联合处理信道估计与MIMO检测。

Result: 仿真验证了所提出的AI-ICED接收机结合SI-DMRS方案相比采用非重叠DMRS和数据符号的传统系统，能够有效地提升频谱效率。

Conclusion: SI-DMRS传输结合AI-ICED接收机是一种有效的频谱效率增强方案，解析框架为系统参数优化提供了理论指导，Transformer编码器架构与ICED结构的结合展现了AI在物理层接收机设计中的潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider transmissions with superimposed (SI) demodulation-reference-symbol (DMRS) and data in orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) based multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. First, we derive an analytical framework to characterize the iterative behavior between the mean-square errors (MSEs) of channel estimation (CE) and MIMO detection (MD) within an iterative CE and detection (ICED) process. This framework is subsequently utilized to optimize power allocation and pilot patterns between the DMRS and data symbols for SI-DMRS transmission. Second, we design an artificial intelligence (AI) based receiver built upon Transformer encoders for SI-DMRS transmissions, which incorporates an iterative CE and detection (ICED) structure. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed AI-ICED receiver, combined with SI-DMRS, effectively increases spectral efficiency (SE) compared to conventional systems using non-overlapped DMRS and data symbols.

</details>


### [104] [The Capacity Region of the Multiple Access Channel with Non-Signaling Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13860)
*Yuhang Yao,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了K发送者离散无记忆多址接入信道（MAC）在非信号（NS）辅助下的容量区域，证明其形式与经典MAC容量区域相同，仅允许发送者间输入分布任意依赖。此外，通过构造实例证明NS辅助的乘性信道容量增益可任意接近K，结合先前上界，确立了K为所有K发送者MAC设置中NS辅助乘性增益的极值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子信息论中，非信号（NS）辅助为通信信道提供了一种超越经典能力的能力度量。此前Fawzi和Fermé仅针对K=2发送者的MAC建立了NS辅助容量区域的外界，将这一结果推广到任意K个发送者，并确定NS辅助增益的极值，是自然且重要的理论缺口。

Method: 作者对K发送者MAC在NS辅助下的容量区域进行了完全刻画，表明其与经典MAC容量区域形式一致，但输入分布允许跨发送者任意依赖。同时构造了具体K发送者MAC实例，以展示NS辅助带来的乘性增益可以任意接近K。

Result: 证明了K发送者NS辅助MAC容量区域与经典形式一致（输入分布任意依赖），并给出了乘性增益可任意逼近K的实例；结合先前工作的上界，确定K为NS辅助乘性增益在所有K发送者MAC中的极值。

Conclusion: 本文完成了K发送者MAC在NS辅助下容量区域的完整刻画，并确立了NS辅助乘性增益的极值为K，从而为多发送者通信场景中非信号资源的价值提供了理论界限。

Abstract: The capacity region of the $K$-sender discrete memoryless multiple access channel (MAC) is fully characterized when non-signaling (NS) assistance is available to all $K$ transmitters and the receiver. It is shown to have the same form as the classical capacity region of the MAC, except that the input distribution is allowed to be arbitrarily dependent across the senders. In particular, the NS-assisted capacity region matches the natural generalization to $K$ senders of an outer bound that was previously established by Fawzi and Fermé for $K=2$ senders. Additionally, we provide examples of $K$-sender MACs where the multiplicative gain in capacity from NS-assistance is arbitrarily close to $K$. Combined with an upper bound from prior work, this establishes $K$ as the extremal value of the multiplicative gain from NS-assistance across all $K$-sender MAC settings.

</details>


### [105] [New lower bounds for constant-weight codes via seeded bit-swap tabu search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13906)
*William Echols*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文使用带种子初始化的比特交换禁忌搜索算法，为二进制常权码的最大规模A(n,d,w)找到了124个新的下界构造，并由此改进了kissing numbers的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制常权码的最大规模A(n,d,w)是一个重要的组合优化问题，其下界的改进对编码理论及相关领域（如球填充、kissing numbers）有直接推动作用。传统的搜索方法可能陷入局部最优，需要更有效的启发式算法来发现新的更好构造。

Method: 采用种子初始化（seeded initialization）与比特交换禁忌搜索（bit-swap tabu search）相结合的启发式算法，在参数空间(n,d,w)中搜索能够改进现有下界的常权码构造。该算法通过禁忌表避免循环搜索，并通过种子解引导搜索方向以提高效率。

Result: 发现了124个新的构造，改进了A(n,d,w)的现有下界。特别地，对A(n,8,8)在n∈{32,33,34,37}时获得了更强的界，作为推论进一步改进了kissing numbers τ32、τ33、τ34和τ37的下界。

Conclusion: 比特交换禁忌搜索与种子初始化的组合是一种有效的搜索策略，能够为二进制常权码下界问题提供大量新构造，并且这些改进在相关数学问题（如kissing numbers）中具有连锁效应。

Abstract: A binary constant-weight code is a set of binary words of length $n$ such that each word has exactly weight $w$ and is at least Hamming distance $d$ from every other word in the set. $A(n,d,w)$ denotes the maximum size of a binary constant-weight code with parameters $(n,d,w)$. Using seeded initialization with bit-swap tabu search, we found 124 new constructions that improve existing lower bounds for $A(n,d,w)$. As a corollary of stronger bounds on $A(n,8,8)$ for $n \in \{ 32,33,34,37 \}$, we also improve lower bounds on kissing numbers $τ_{32}$, $τ_{33}$, $τ_{34}$, and $τ_{37}$.

</details>


### [106] [A Structural Characterization of Entropy Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13917)
*Daniel Lazarev*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个测度论框架来刻画"可容许"的熵泛函，要求当输入测度关于参考测度绝对连续时，输入熵有界于参考熵。在广义均值复合下，作者完整刻画了所有此类熵泛函，得到由均值生成元、熵尺度和可加性假设逐层决定的四级层次结构。核心判据是：连续严格单调生成元g可容许当且仅当t↦g(1/t)对递增g严格凸（对递减g严格凹）。该判据与Csiszár f-散度生成元的严格凸性等价，从而在马尔可夫核下保证数据处理不等式及精确等式条件。乘积可加性唯一确定Rényi族，内部可加性或乘积可加性加算术均值复合唯一确定Shannon熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon、Rényi、Tsallis等熵族在统计学中被广泛使用（如最大熵推断、最小散度估计、拟合优度检验），但选择哪种熵往往取决于惯例而非结构性的公理原则。作者希望建立一个基于"可容许性"（admissibility）的测度论框架，从结构性原理而非任意约定出发来区分和刻画不同的熵泛函，并解决Rényi于1961年提出的关于何种广义均值可替代算术均值的问题。

Method: 作者引入测度论框架，定义可容许性条件：若输入测度关于参考测度绝对连续，则熵有界性必须成立。在广义均值复合（generalized mean-value composition）的设定下，利用生成元函数g的凸性/凹性分析来刻画所有可容许的熵泛函。通过判定条件t↦g(1/t)的严格凸（对递增g）或严格凹（对递减g），建立与Csiszár f-散度生成元的关系，并在此基础上依次施加乘积可加性和内部可加性假设，构建四级层次结构。

Result: 作者获得了完整的可容许熵泛函的特征刻画，建立了四级层次结构（由均值生成元、熵尺度、可加性假设依次决定）。核心结果包括：Rényi族被乘积可加性唯一选出，Shannon熵被内部可加性或"乘积可加性+算术均值复合"唯一选出。该判据同时等价于Csiszár f-散度生成元的严格凸性，保证了马尔可夫核下的数据处理不等式及精确等式条件。此外，构造性方法生成了新的可容许熵与散度族，包括积分变换实例。

Conclusion: 本文为熵泛函的选择提供了严格的测度论基础，将以往基于惯例的熵族选择转化为基于可容许性公理的确定性层次结构。这不仅解决了Rényi的经典开放问题，还建立了熵可容许性、f-散度的凸性和数据处理不等式之间的深层联系，并提供了构造新熵族的系统性方法。

Abstract: Entropy functionals and their associated divergences underlie many statistical methods, including maximum entropy inference, minimum divergence estimation, and goodness-of-fit testing, yet choosing among Shannon, Rényi, Tsallis, and more general entropies is often a matter of convention rather than structural principle. We introduce a measure theoretic framework in which admissibility requires the entropy of an input measure to be bounded above by that of its reference measure whenever the former is absolutely continuous with respect to the latter. Under generalized mean-value composition, we characterize all such entropy functionals and obtain a four-level hierarchy determined successively by the mean generator, entropy scale, and additivity assumptions. A continuous strictly monotone generator $g$ is admissible exactly when $t\mapsto g(1/t)$ is strictly convex for increasing $g$, or strictly concave for decreasing $g$. This resolves a question posed by Rényi (Proc. 4th Berkeley Sympos. Math. Statist. Prob., 1961) concerning which generalized means may replace the arithmetic mean in his entropy axiomatization. The same criterion is equivalent to strict convexity of an associated Csiszár $f$-divergence generator and therefore yields data processing under Markov kernels with an exact equality condition. Within this hierarchy, product additivity singles out the Rényi family, while internal additivity, or product additivity together with arithmetic mean-value composition, singles out Shannon entropy. The characterization is constructive and yields new admissible entropy and divergence families, including integral-transform examples.

</details>


### [107] [Integrated Information in the Active Inference Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14165)
*Carlotta Langer,Jesse van Oostrum,Nihat Ay*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文结合主动推断框架与整合信息理论，定义了一种针对生成模型的整合信息度量方法，并通过仿真实验发现整合信息度量与主动推断智能体的自由能之间存在相关性，且该相关性随生成模型规模增大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推断框架为建模感知和行动选择提供了统一原则，但缺乏从意识/整合信息角度分析智能体的理论工具。作者希望将整合信息理论（IIT）引入主动推断智能体分析，从而量化智能体生成模型的整合信息水平。

Method: 将主动推断框架与整合信息理论（IIT）结合，通过对生成模型添加额外的结构性假设，定义了一种适用于生成模型的整合信息度量；随后通过仿真模拟智能体进行实验验证。

Result: 仿真实验表明，整合信息度量与主动推断智能体的自由能之间存在相关性，且该相关性随着生成模型规模的增大而增强。

Conclusion: 主动推断智能体的生成模型可以在整合信息理论的框架下被量化分析，整合信息与自由能之间的相关性为理解意识相关机制与主动推断过程的关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The active inference framework provides a principled approach to modeling sentient behavior. In this framework perception and action selection are treated in a unified way. The resulting agents form an internal generative model of the relevant dynamics of the world in order to infer their future observations, their internal states and to select actions. We combine this modeling framework with the Integrated Information Theory of consciousness and are therefore able to analyze the active inference agents from the perspective of integrated information. The Integrated Information Theory aims at quantifying the level of consciousness of a system by assessing its capability to integrate information. Here, we define a measure of integrated information for the generative model by making an additional structural assumption. Experiments with simulated agents reveal a correlation between integrated information measures and the free energy of the active inference agents that increases with the size of the generative model.

</details>


### [108] [Positioning with Flexible Reflectors: Solution and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14184)
*Jiajun He,Han Yu,Danyan Lin,Gaofeng Pan,Hing Cheung So,Stefano Buzzi,Hien Quoc Ngo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究柔性反射面（FR）辅助的目标定位问题，针对视距（LoS）路径不可用的场景，利用FR产生的虚拟LoS路径实现目标定位，并推导了克拉美-罗下界（CRLB）用于优化FR的部署位置和方向，同时分析了随机部署下的CRLB分布，为FR部署和网络设计提供理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LoS不可用的严重阻塞场景下，传统目标定位方法性能严重下降。柔性反射面（FR）作为低成本、高能效的方案可重塑电磁传播环境，但其在定位应用中的性能极限和部署优化问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 利用FR生成的虚拟LoS路径构建简单而准确的目标定位估计器；推导CRLB作为性能极限度量；基于CRLB优化FR的位置和方向；考虑随机FR部署，表征CRLB的统计分布特性。

Result: 仿真结果表明所提出的方案性能接近CRLB下界，推导的解析结果能够有效指导FR部署和网络配置设计。

Conclusion: FR群辅助定位在严重阻塞场景下是可行的，所提出的基于虚拟LoS路径的方案性能接近理论极限，且CRLB分析可用于FR部署的优化指导。

Abstract: Flexible reflectors (FRs) have emerged as a low-cost and energy-efficient solution for reshaping electromagnetic propagation environments across a wide range of applications. This paper investigates FR-swarm-assisted target localization in scenarios where line-of-sight (LoS) paths are unavailable. By leveraging the virtual LoS paths created by the FRs, a simple yet accurate estimator is proposed for localization under severe blockage conditions. To characterize the performance limits of the proposed scheme, we derive the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) and use it to optimize the positions and orientations of the FRs. Furthermore, by accounting for random FR deployment, we characterize the CRLB distribution and reveal how different network configurations affect localization accuracy. Simulation results demonstrate that the developed scheme closely approaches the CRLB performance, while the derived analytical results provide useful guidelines for FR deployment and network design.

</details>


### [109] [Lossy Compression via Sparse Regression Codes: Generalized Construction and Finite-length Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14494)
*Galen Reeves,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏回归码（SPARCs）在基于简单贪婪编码规则（相关法与距离法）下的有损压缩问题。作者推广了SPARC构造，提出了一类"加法正交回归码"（标准SPARC为其特例），并推导了关于平方误差失真的非渐近界。结果显示功率分配在控制失真中起关键作用，优化后的功率分配改善了SPARC的有限长度压缩性能，同时该界也为低复杂度变体（如符号SPARC和K稀疏SPARC）提供了失真保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPARCs最初为有损压缩设计时依赖于次优的贪婪编码规则，其有限长度失真行为尚不清晰。作者希望通过推广SPARC构造并推导非渐近界，深入理解功率分配对失真的影响，从而优化压缩性能并为低复杂度变体提供理论保证。

Method: 论文将标准SPARC推广为"加法正交回归码"类，涵盖相关法和距离法两种贪婪编码规则。通过追踪编码残差在各阶段间的演化过程，推导平方误差失真的非渐近上界，并据此基于编码参数优化功率分配策略。

Result: 优化后的功率分配提升了SPARC的有限长度压缩性能；推导出的失真界对符号SPARC和K稀疏SPARC等低复杂度变体同样成立，提供了相应的失真保证。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了功率分配在贪婪编码SPARC类中的关键作用，为高维有损压缩场景下设计高效且可分析的回归码提供了理论基础，同时扩展了SPARC变体的适用范围。

Abstract: We study sparse regression codes (SPARCs) for lossy compression under simple greedy encoding rules, including both correlation-based and distance-based methods. We generalize the SPARC construction, and consider the class of \emph{additive orthogonal} regression codes, of which standard SPARCs are a special case. For this class of codes, we derive nonasymptotic bounds on the squared-error distortion by tracking the evolution of the encoding residual across stages. Our results highlight the role of power allocation in controlling the distortion, allowing us to optimize the allocation based on the parameters of the code. The optimized allocation improves the finite-length compression performance of SPARCs, and our bounds provide distortion guarantees for lower complexity variants of SPARCs, like signed SPARCs and $K$-sparse SPARCs.

</details>


### [110] [Learning-to-Transition for Large-scale and High-Order MIMO Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14511)
*Yubo Zhang,Yiyao Liu,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出学习到转移（L2T）框架，将高维MIMO检测建模为完整向量转移的随机序列，通过信道耦合Transformer更新嵌入与采样策略，结合块状自回归分解捕捉流间依赖。硬输出阶段采用残差到BER的课程学习训练转移网络，软输出阶段通过参数级克隆将硬策略迁移至未绑定的软输入软输出迭代检测解码接收机，并利用贝叶斯准则倾斜候选生成，最终产生后验与外禀LLR供LDPC解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶MIMO检测需要在巨大的离散符号空间中高效搜索，同时产生可靠的软信息用于信道解码。传统方法难以兼顾搜索效率与软输出质量，因此需要一种能够同时支持硬判决与软判决的统一检测框架。

Method: 1) 将MIMO检测建模为完整向量转移的随机序列；2) 信道耦合Transformer在每次转移中同时更新实例嵌入与采样策略；3) 块状自回归分解以中等顺序复杂度捕捉跨流依赖；4) 硬输出阶段采用残差到BER的课程学习，先学习精确残差度量的搜索几何再对齐传输比特准确率；5) 软输出阶段将硬策略参数级克隆到IDD接收机的每一层（绑定性到未绑定性转移）；6) 解码器先验按贝叶斯规则倾斜候选生成，似然加权终端假设产生后验与外禀LLR；7) 多阶段训练策略通过合成与循环解码器生成的先验渐进暴露接收机，稳定硬到软的迁移。

Result: 该框架在硬输出检测中通过残差到BER课程学习获得有效的搜索策略，在软输出接收中通过绑定到未绑定的参数级转移保留零先验搜索动力学，同时允许各层与各轮在解码反馈下专门化，最终为LDPC解码提供高质量的外禀信息。

Conclusion: L2T框架为MIMO检测提供了一种统一的学习驱动解决方案，从硬输出搜索到软输出迭代解码的全链路设计，通过课程学习、参数级策略迁移和多阶段训练，在保持高效搜索的同时实现可靠的软信息生成。

Abstract: High-order multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) detection requires efficient search over a large discrete symbol space while producing reliable soft information for channel decoding. This paper develops a learning-to-transition (L2T) framework that formulates MIMO detection as a stochastic sequence of complete-vector transitions. At each transition, a channel-coupled Transformer updates both the instance embedding and the sampling policy, while a blockwise autoregressive factorization captures inter-stream dependence with moderate sequential complexity. For hard-output detection, a transition network is applied recursively and trained through a residual-to-BER curriculum, which first learns the MIMO search geometry from the exact residual metric and then aligns the policy with transmitted-bit accuracy. For soft-output reception, the well-trained hard policy is cloned at the parameter level into every layer of an untied soft-input soft-output iterative detection and decoding (IDD) receiver. This tied-to-untied transfer preserves the learned zero-prior search dynamics while enabling layer- and round-specific specialization under decoder feedback. Within each IDD round, decoder priors tilt candidate generation according to Bayes' rule, and likelihood-weighted terminal hypotheses produce posterior and extrinsic log-likelihood ratios for LDPC decoding. A multi-stage training strategy further stabilizes the hard-to-soft transfer by progressively exposing the receiver to synthetic and in-loop decoder-generated priors.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Benchmarking data-driven material models on the classic Treloar dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14063)
*Hagen Holthusen,Moritz Flaschel,Denisa Martonová,Ellen Kuhl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用Treloar经典实验数据，对六种机器学习超弹性本构建模方法（广义不变量CANNs、物理增强神经网络、自适应材料指纹识别、高效无监督本构定律识别与发现等）进行系统基准测试，比较其拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性和实现难度，并讨论了预测准确性与模型复杂性之间的权衡。结果表明所有方法均能很好复现基准数据，无单一最优方法，各方法各有优劣，作者提供了公开的源代码和对比结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习正在深刻改变本构建模领域，涌现出大量基于学习的方法，但缺乏对这些方法的系统性对比研究。研究人员面对众多候选方法时，难以知晓它们在实践中的真实表现差异，需要一份客观、可复现的基准测试来指导方法选择。

Method: 以Treloar经典橡胶超弹性实验数据为统一基准，对六种主流机器学习超弹性建模框架进行系统对比：（1）广义不变量的本构人工神经网络（CANNs）、（2）物理增强神经网络（PANN）、（3）自适应材料指纹识别、（4）高效无监督本构定律识别与发现（EULICID）等。从拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性、实现难度四个维度评估，并通过材料参数数量与模型导数计算时间来量化模型复杂度与预测精度之间的权衡。

Result: 所有六种方法均能以优秀精度复现Treloar基准数据。但不同方法在拟合性能、计算开销、超参数鲁棒性和实现便捷性上各有差异，没有单一方法全面胜出。研究揭示了预测精度与模型复杂性之间的内在权衡关系。

Conclusion: 不存在在所有维度上都最优的"万金油"方法，研究者应根据具体应用场景（如对计算效率、实现便捷性或模型可解释性的需求）选择合适的方法。作者公开了全部源代码、数据和对比脚本，为后续研究和实际应用提供了可复现的参考基准。

Abstract: Machine learning is rapidly reshaping constitutive modeling, offers new ways to learn material behavior directly from experimental data, and challenges long-established modeling paradigms. But with a growing number of machine-learning-based approaches available, how do they compare in practice? In this paper, we use the classic experimental data of Treloar to benchmark popular frameworks for hyperelasticity: (Generalized-Invariant) Constitutive Artificial Neural Networks, Physics-Augmented Neural Networks, (Adaptive) Material Fingerprinting, and Efficient Unsupervised Constitutive Law Identification & Discovery. We compare their fitting performance, computational cost, hyperparameter sensitivity, and ease of implementation. Furthermore, we discuss the trade-offs between predictive accuracy and model complexity. The latter is assessed by quantifying both the number of material parameters in the discovered models and the computational time required to evaluate the constitutive model and its derivatives. The results show that all methods can reproduce the benchmark data remarkably well. Rather than identifying a single winner, we highlight the strengths and limitations of each approach and provide practical guidance for their use. The source code for all six methods, including the training and comparison scripts, as well as all results and data used in this study, is publicly available via https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21915635.

</details>


### [112] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RubricForge通过反射式进化从少量带标签的轨迹中自动生成可读的评分标准，用冻结的7B模型作为agent和judge，相比手工编写的G-Eval，虽然原始一致性和分数校准上无显著优势，但将假阳性率（错误通过失败轨迹）降低了约一半（0.115 vs 0.173），对于无环境奖励的部署场景更具可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型agent时，通常依赖另一个语言模型作为自动裁判，但现有裁判（如G-Eval手工编写rubric或微调裁判权重）倾向于将流畅但不成功的轨迹误判为成功。作者希望从少量带有真实奖励标注的轨迹中自动归纳出评分标准，使裁判结果可归因、可信任。

Method: RubricForge采用反射式进化（reflective evolution），在带标签的轨迹集合上迭代优化裁判评分标准，使其与环境的真实奖励最大化一致，然后冻结该标准，在无需环境访问的情况下，通过单次模型调用对留出轨迹进行评分。优化产物是人类可读文本，每条判定都可归因于命名的标准。

Result: 在tau-bench（220条rollout抽取173条标注轨迹）和WebShop（160条轨迹）上，与通用G-Eval裁判相比，原始一致性无显著优势（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略微逊色（|err|差异-0.048，p=2x10^-4），但在tau-bench上假阳性率显著降低（0.115 vs 0.173），且WebShop分级结果Spearman相关系数更高（0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对于无奖励信号的评估器而言，假阳性率而非总体一致性才是部署相关的重要指标，因为假阳性会发布有缺陷的agent，而假阴性仅需重试。RubricForge的核心增益在于保真度（有效性），而非原始一致性。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [113] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B混合专家模型（40个MoE层，每层256个专家，top-8路由）进行了系统的层敏感度分析。核心发现：层敏感性具有强深度依赖性——早期（0-9）和中期（10-29）层对专家掩码高度脆弱，而晚期层（30-39）尤其很晚期层（35-39）能容忍激进的低幅度专家掩码。晚期聚焦策略在XLCoST代码翻译基准上以掩码640个专家保留419/500个Good+Similar输出，远超均匀掩码策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在保持计算效率的同时扩展LLM规模被广泛采用，但各MoE层的相对重要性尚未被充分刻画，尤其在模型压缩场景下缺乏系统的层敏感度分析。现有压缩方法通常采用均匀或启发式的专家掩码策略，没有考虑层深度对压缩敏感性的影响，因此需要建立depth-aware的专家掩码实证基础。

Method: 采用基于幅度的专家掩码方法，在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层、256专家/层、top-8路由）上进行系统性层敏感度分析。实验在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上开展，涵盖100、300和500个prompt三个评估规模，部署于三台H100 GPU服务器。比较了均匀全层掩码与不同层深度聚焦策略（早期、中期、晚期、很晚期）在不同掩码率下的质量保持效果，并额外探测了top-k路由宽度从8降至6的影响。

Result: 均匀30%全层掩码在300-prompt规模下仅保留150/300个Good+Similar输出；而晚期聚焦策略（layer 30-39）掩码640-1,145个专家即可保留249-255/300。在500-prompt留出验证集上，很晚期窄策略（layer 35-39 @ 50%）取得最佳质量/掩码专家权衡，保留419/500个Good+Similar而仅掩码10,240个专家中的640个。此外，top-k路由从8降至6在100-prompt探测中产生显著的墙钟时间减少且无Good+Similar损失，但尚不能与激进专家掩码清晰组合。

Conclusion: 层敏感度在MoE模型中表现强深度依赖性，晚期层（尤其很晚期层）对专家掩码高度鲁棒，为depth-aware MoE专家掩码提供了实证基础。研究成果为物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分以及基于训练恢复的实际压缩路径建立了基础。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [114] [Attributing Preprocessing Invariance in Spectral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14227)
*Dongjun Wei,Hongyi Wu,Yinuo Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了光谱基础模型中"预处理不变性"的评估方式。作者以拉曼光谱基础模型为例，发现模型在输入归一化后会将某些不同预处理得到的谱图映射到相同向量，导致编码器收到的输入完全相同，因此"不变性"并非来自学习。实验表明该模型在六个评估数据集上并不优于其自带的归一化本身，训练对编码器虽有提升，但多个发布的系统宣称的"学习到的不变性"实际上归因于无参数的归一化操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预处理不变性评估方法（训练/测试使用不同预处理管道）可能产生误导：当归一化本身就把不同预处理谱图映射到相同向量时，"保精度"并不能证明模型学到了不变性。作者旨在厘清不变性究竟来自学习还是来自无参数的输入归一化。

Method: 以拉曼基础模型为案例，分析其输入归一化的性质：当一个谱图是另一个谱图的正倍数加常数时，基于谱图自身统计量的归一化会将其映射为同一向量，而多种标准预处理操作恰好具有该形式。作者将编码器与"仅归一化"进行对比评测，在六个拉曼数据集上检验模型是否超越纯归一化；同时设计了受控实验（让变换只在到达编码器时才被学习）以及数值测试来判定归一化移除哪些变换。

Result: 在六个拉曼评估数据集上，模型相比其自身归一化没有可测量提升；相比原始谱图虽有所改善，但归一化本身也能做到。训练确实改进了编码器（优于随机初始化），但受控实验表明模型只在变换到达编码器时才会学习忽略它。跨五个模态的多个发布系统中，多数归一化已能移除这类变换，且部分系统将之宣称归因于学习。复现其中两个系统的对比也未见增益。

Conclusion: 光谱基础模型宣称的"预处理不变性"在相当程度上可能并非来自学习，而是来自无参数归一化操作的固有属性。评估协议应将模型与"纯归一化基线"进行对比，否则会高估模型学到的不变能力。

Abstract: Preprocessing invariance is an appealing goal for spectral foundation models: a frozen model should remain useful when laboratories preprocess spectra differently. It is usually measured by training a classifier under one preprocessing pipeline and testing it under another, with preserved accuracy read as evidence of learning. We revisit that reading, using a Raman foundation model as a case study. Such models normalize their inputs before any learned parameter is applied. If that normalization maps two differently preprocessed spectra to the same vector, the encoder receives identical inputs, so the invariance cannot be attributed to learning. For a normalization that uses each spectrum's own statistics, this happens exactly when one spectrum is a positive multiple of the other plus a constant. Several standard preprocessing operations take that form. The encoder should therefore be measured against the normalization alone, which has no learned parameters. On six Raman evaluation datasets, the model does not measurably outperform its own normalization. It improves on raw spectra, but so does the normalization alone. Training does improve the encoder over random initialization, and a controlled experiment shows that it learns to ignore a transformation only when that transformation reaches it. A numerical test settles which transformations a given normalization removes. Across released systems in five modalities, most normalizations already remove transformations of that form, and several of those systems claim that invariance as learned. Replicating the comparison on two of them shows no gain either.

</details>


### [115] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过电路分析46个覆盖语言、形式推理、社会推理和物理推理四个认知领域的任务，发现大语言模型（LLMs）发展出了与人类大脑相似的模块化架构——在人类中调用相同网络的任务在LLM中招募重叠的神经元，而调用不同网络的任务则招募不同的神经元，表明模块化组织可能是智能系统的普遍属性而非生物大脑的偶然产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑表现出显著的功能模块化（语言、形式推理、心智推理、物理推理各有独立网络），但这一组织方式是否是构建智能系统的根本原则，还是生物大脑进化中的偶然事件，尚不清楚。作者试图通过考察另一类智能系统（LLM）是否也涌现出类似模块化来回答这一问题。

Method: 对N=46个跨四个认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的任务进行电路分析，对比LLM中神经元激活模式与人类大脑网络招募模式的对应关系，检验任务在同一人类网络中共用LLM神经元、在不同人类网络中使用不同神经元的现象。

Result: LLM中确实涌现出与人类大脑相似的模块化架构：在人类中调用相同网络的任务在LLM中招募重叠的神经元，而调用不同人类网络的任务则在LLM中招募不同的神经元，表明模块化结构在大脑与神经网络中趋同涌现。

Conclusion: 大脑与神经网络中模块化的趋同涌现表明，模块化组织可能是智能系统的一个根本属性，而非特定于生物大脑的进化偶然事件。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [116] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于Chutes平台一年的生产环境trace数据，从全局特征化和纵向研究两个角度深入分析了真实世界LLM服务负载，揭示了时间演化、用户-模型交互结构等传统聚合视图下隐藏的模式，并将发布完整的一年trace以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务负载研究在规模和范围上存在局限，通常观察时间短、对生产环境中用户与模型交互方式的可见性有限，无法充分捕捉LLM服务负载随时间演化的规律以及用户-模型交互如何塑造生产流量。

Method: 基于Chutes平台一年的生产trace数据，从聚合（aggregate）、时间（temporal）、模型级（model-level）和用户级（user-level）四个视角进行分析，覆盖流行模型和长尾模型，研究负载演化及用户-模型结构。

Result: 揭示了传统聚合视图下通常被隐藏的工作负载演化模式与用户-模型交互结构，并承诺随论文发布完整一年的生产trace数据。

Conclusion: 通过与已有研究不同的全量生产数据覆盖，进一步加深了对真实世界LLM服务负载的理解，并为未来服务系统研究和基准测试提供了完整、未经采样或合成处理的生产trace资源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [117] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentao，一个面向工具使用型LLM代理的受治理的本地优先运行时系统。它通过分层架构将模型生成的行动提案与主机授权的执行分离，使权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹成为显式的运行时抽象，从而提升代理的可治理性、可检查性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理作为执行系统会调用工具、修改本地状态、使用持久记忆并与外部协议交互，这些能力带来过度权限、弱审计性、提示注入、工具投毒和不受控制的副作用等安全风险。需要一个能将这些风险显式管理的运行时系统。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构，包括主机面向层（host-facing surfaces）、主机契约（host contract）、运行时核心（runtime core）、权限中介工具系统，以及支持记忆、重放、插件、技能、子代理和协议集成的子系统。系统将模型生成的行动提案与主机授权的执行分离，并设计了治理模型、执行流水线和结构化事件接口。

Result: Agentao不提供形式化的安全保证，而是演示了如何将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹转化为显式的运行时抽象，从而构建更可控、可检查和适合主机控制的本地环境的代理系统。代码已在GitHub公开发布。

Conclusion: Agentao的贡献在于提出一种治理优先的设计范式：通过运行时架构层面的显式封装来增强LLM代理的可治理性和可审计性，为构建更安全的代理系统提供了具体的设计参考和开源实现。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [118] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张当前以"超人自主性能"为主导的AI评估范式（隐含以取代人类为目标）正在引导AI发展走向错误方向。作者认为AI社区应转向评估"人机协作团队"的性能，以促进AI系统成为人类能力的真正补充，从而带来更好的社会结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估范式聚焦于超人自主性能，实质上以替代人类为目标，这种评估方式正在误导AI发展走向错误方向，可能导致不良的社会后果。

Method: 本文采用立场论文（position paper）形式，通过论证分析提出应将AI评估从"替代人类"的单一性能指标转向"人机协作团队性能"评估的新范式。

Result: 作者提出协作式评估转变将培育真正补充人类能力的AI系统，从而比当前评估范式产生更好的社会结果（此为论点主张，非实证结果）。

Conclusion: AI评估范式应从追求超人自主性能转向人机协作团队性能的评估，这一转变将引导AI系统成为人类能力的真正补充，实现更好的社会效益。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [119] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中"稳定型误校准"现象——高置信度错误答案在微小扰动下仍保持局部稳定，而非仅表现为脆弱的内部推理。通过输出层审计评分与内部敏感度探测两个诊断手段，发现自我批判提示可降低隐藏层敏感度（提示诱导的局部稳定化），但审计定义的过度自信错误并不比正确回答更具局部敏感性，说明某些高置信度错误是稳定且误校准的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM中的高置信度错误常被视为内部推理脆弱性的证据，但作者提出另一种可能性：这些错误可能是"稳定误校准"——即在局部扰动下依然稳定的错误答案。这挑战了将高置信错误简单归因于推理不稳定的主流观点。

Method: 结合两种诊断工具：(1) 标签感知的输出层审计分数，基于强制回答基线比较变异性和过度自信错误来对领域排序；(2) 内部敏感度探针，测量隐藏状态在扰动下的移动。在多元二分类事实审计集上，使用三种开源权重模型进行验证。

Result: 审计分数能够追踪自我批判在何时降低决策损失（尽管直接标注基线排名更强）；自我批判提示在三个开源模型各层一致降低隐藏状态敏感度；但审计定义的过度自信错误与自信正确答案在局部敏感性上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 自我批判提示诱导了局部稳定化而非单纯的输出层弃权模式，但这并不意味着校准：部分高置信度错误可能是稳定且误校准的，而非简单脆弱的，需区分"脆弱性"与"误校准"两种错误类型。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference.
