The efficacy of ScienceFlow further demonstrates that efficient state management, adaptive exploration, and objective-aligned execution are critical for scaling autonomous research beyond short-horizon interactions.

</details>


### [186] [Disentangled Shared Representations Improve Morpho-Transcriptomic Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14355)
*Julian Ostermaier,Swann Ruyter,Reuben Dorent,Daniel Racoceanu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统地比较了标准与显式解缠（disentangled）的多模态表示学习方法在空间转录组学（ST）与H&E组织学配对数据上的表现，发现对比学习目标优于VAE方法，且解缠变体在重构与探测指标上有所提升，虽然收益依赖于模型家族、任务、方向和解缠强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学能够同时测量基因表达和组织形态，为学习捕获共享形态-转录组结构的多模态表示提供了机会。但现有标准多模态模型通常将模态压缩到公共潜在空间，未显式分离共享与模态特异的变异来源，可能限制下游应用的效果。作者因此探究显式解缠共享与私有潜变量是否可以改善配对H&E与ST数据的多模态表示学习。

Method: 在匹配的实验条件下，对比了两类方法——基于VAE的方法和对比学习方法，每种方法分别使用标准变体和解缠变体，在两个癌症队列上进行评估。表示质量通过跨模态重构、下游探测（downstream probing）以及跨模态探测迁移（cross-modal probe transfer）三个维度进行评估。

Result: 实验揭示了两个主要趋势：(1) 对比目标在下游探测性能上优于基于VAE的模型；(2) 解缠变体改善了所选择的重构和探测指标，但收益取决于模型家族、具体任务、方向以及解缠强度等因素。

Conclusion: 显式分解共享和模态特异信息能够改善空间转录组学的多模态表示学习，同时该研究提供了一个对未来基础模型有用的评估框架。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables the simultaneous profiling of gene expression and tissue morphology, creating an opportunity to learn multimodal representations capturing shared morpho-transcriptomic structure. However, standard multimodal models often compress modalities into a common latent space without explicitly separating shared and modality-specific sources of variation, which may limit downstream utility. We investigate whether explicit disentanglement of shared and private latent components improves multimodal representation learning for paired Hematoxylin \& Eosin (H\&E) and ST data. We compare VAE-based and contrastive approaches, each in standard and disentangled variants, across two cancer cohorts under matched experimental conditions. Representations are evaluated using cross-modal reconstruction, downstream probing and cross-modal probe transfer. The experiments suggest two main trends. First, contrastive objectives yield higher downstream probing performance than VAE-based models. Second, disentangled variants improve the selected reconstruction and probing metrics, although the gains depend on the model family, task, direction, and disentanglement strength. Overall, our results suggest that explicitly factorizing shared and modality-specific information can improve multimodal representation learning for spatial transcriptomics and provides a useful evaluation framework for future foundation models.

</details>


### [187] [Designing Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service using Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14359)
*Keya Patel,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Fattah,Deepak Kanneganti,Aneesh Krishna,Mufti Mahmud,Monowar Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SFLaaS的碳约束神经架构搜索框架，旨在联邦学习即服务（FLaaS）环境中解决消费者可持续性约束导致的不稳定联邦训练问题。该框架通过需求驱动搜索空间、消费者级碳可行性评估和可持续消费者调度策略，在硬碳约束下联合优化预测性能、消费者可行性和参与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLaaS消费者的可持续性约束给在碳可行条件下维持联邦训练带来了重大挑战，这些约束常常导致消费者参与不可行，以及在硬碳约束下产生不稳定的联邦训练。

Method: 1）提出需求驱动的搜索空间，将消费者可持续性画像转化为联邦执行前的可行架构区域；2）开发消费者级碳可行性估计机制，在动态碳条件下评估候选架构；3）提出可持续消费者调度策略，自适应选择可行消费者并分配本地工作负载，以保持消费者参与和统计数据覆盖；4）采用进化搜索策略，在硬碳约束下联合优化预测性能、消费者可行性和参与覆盖率。

Result: 在真实数据集和模拟环境上的实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SFLaaS通过将碳约束感知融入神经架构搜索和消费者调度，为FLaaS环境提供了在可持续性约束下保持联邦训练稳定性和可行性的解决方案，同时兼顾预测性能、消费者参与和数据覆盖。

Abstract: The sustainability constraints of FLaaS consumers pose significant challenges to maintaining carbon-feasible federated training in FLaaS environments. These constraints often lead to infeasible consumer participation and unstable federated training under hard carbon constraints. We propose a Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service (SFLaaS), a carbon- constrained Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework for heteroge- neous sustainable constraints. We introduce a requirement-driven search space that transforms consumer sustainability profiles into a feasible architecture region before federated execution. We develop a consumer-level carbon feasibility estimation mechanism to evaluate candidate architectures under dynamic carbon conditions. We propose a sustainable con- sumer scheduling strategy that adaptively selects feasible consumers and allocates local workloads to preserve consumer participation and statistical data coverage. An evolutionary search strategy jointly optimised for predictive performance, consumer feasibility, and participation coverage under hard carbon constraints. Experiments on real-world datasets and a simulated environment demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [188] [AgentRewind: Recoverable Execution for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14380)
*Yu Zhuang,Kefei Chen,Yitong Duan,Shuxin Zheng,Jian Li,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentRewind是一个运行时恢复框架，通过记录代理上下文和受控环境的对齐检查点，使长时程执行中的LLM智能体能够回退到早期状态并利用先前尝试的信息恢复执行。同时构建了MettleBench基准用于评估长时程工程任务。实验表明AgentRewind在任务成功率和检查清单平均进度上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时程执行中，早期错误会通过上下文和环境状态传播，且后续动作难以逆转。现有方法主要通过计划细化和安全检查来减少错误，但在错误发生后缺乏恢复支持。

Method: 提出AgentRewind运行时恢复框架，记录代理上下文与受控环境的对齐检查点，允许智能体回退到早期状态并携带先前尝试的信息恢复执行；同时构建MettleBench基准用于评估长时程工程任务中的任务完成度和部分进展。

Result: 在多个任务、模型、执行策略和代理框架上的实验表明，AgentRewind相比基线方法在任务成功率和平均检查清单进度上均有提升。

Conclusion: AgentRewind通过运行时状态回退与信息保留有效提升了长时程LLM智能体在错误发生后的恢复能力，是现有预防性方法的有力补充。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks require LLM agents to interact with their environments over long execution horizons. Errors that occur early in execution may propagate through both the agent context and environment state, and their effects may be difficult to reverse through subsequent actions. Existing methods mainly seek to reduce such errors through plan refinement and safety checks but provide little support after errors occur. To enable recovery during long-horizon execution, we present AgentRewind, a runtime recovery framework that records aligned checkpoints of the agent context and controlled environment, allowing agents to return to an earlier state and resume execution with information from previous attempts. We also construct MettleBench, a benchmark for evaluating task completion and partial progress on long-horizon engineering assignments containing a series of related requirements. Experiments across tasks, multiple models, execution strategies, and agent harnesses show that AgentRewind improves task success rate and average checklist progress over the compared baselines.

</details>


### [189] [Tripwire: Triggering Aligned Refusal via Statistically Certified Safety Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14392)
*Wei Zhao,Zhe Li,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tripwire，一种无训练的防御方法，通过逐神经元假设检验（FDR控制）和效用特异性过滤识别安全相关神经元，然后用"触发器式钳制"将其固定在有害条件均值激活上，注入内部有害信号以触发模型的拒答行为。在四种安全对齐LLM和四种代表性攻击下，攻击成功率降至至多2.0%，MT-Bench效用仅下降0.5%-5.3%，为所有防御中最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经元/路径级防御方法在抵御越狱攻击时常显著损害模型效用：一类方法通过抑制有毒神经元来抹除有害语义，但由于有害语义分布在网络中，阻断所有通路需要较大的干预足迹，牺牲过多模型效用；另一类方法使用外部分类器识别安全神经元，但同样会损害对模型效用重要的神经元。此外，现有方法始终开启，即使没有攻击也会扰动所有良性请求。

Method: Tripwire包含两个步骤：(1) 通过逐神经元假设检验（FDR控制）结合效用特异性过滤器，识别安全特异性神经元；(2) 使用触发器式钳制（trigger-style clamp）将选中的神经元固定在有害条件均值激活状态，注入内部有害输入信号以触发对齐过程中学到的拒答行为。该钳制方法可通过两种可证明等价的部署模式实现：检测器门控的推理时干预和离线的偏置补丁权重编辑。

Result: 在四种安全对齐LLM和四种代表性越狱攻击下，Tripwire将平均攻击成功率降至至多2.0%，同时MT-Bench效用仅下降0.5%至5.3%，是所有防御方法中效用损失最小的。

Conclusion: 通过精准识别安全特异性神经元而非广泛抑制或抑制通用神经元，Tripwire实现了对越狱攻击的强大防御与最小化效用损失的兼顾，且支持两种等价的部署模式以增加实用性。

Abstract: Neuron- and path-level interventions offer the finest-grained route to defending large language models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, yet existing methods fall short of this promise, i.e., they often compromise model utility significantly. Specifically, one line of work suppresses toxic neurons to erase harmful semantics, but since such semantics are distributed across the network, blocking every pathway forces a large intervention footprint. An alternative line of research focus on identify safety neurons using external classifiers. While promising, the existing approaches suffer from compromising neurons that are important for the model utility as well. Moreover, both approaches remain always on and thus perturb every benign request even when no attack is present. To address these limitations, we present \ours{}, a training-free defense that first identifies safety-specific neurons through per-neuron hypothesis tests under false-discovery-rate control together with a utility-specificity filter. Based on this identification, a trigger-style clamp holds the selected neurons at their harmful-conditional mean activations, injecting an internal harmful-input signal that triggers the refusal behavior learned during alignment. The clamp is then realized by two provably equivalent deployment modes, namely a detector-gated inference-time intervention and an offline bias-patch weight edit. Extensive experiments across four safety-aligned LLMs and four representative attacks demonstrate that \ours{} reduces the average attack success rate to at most 2.0\% while incurring a utility drop of only 0.5\% to 5.3\% on MT-Bench, the smallest among all defenses. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Tripwire-65C4.

</details>


### [190] [The Past and Future of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14407)
*Ross D. King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI科学家（AI Scientists）的过去与未来——能够自动化科学发现的机器系统。论文回顾了从Adam（首个通过假设形成与物理实验循环做出新颖科学发现的机器）到Eve（现代自驱动实验室架构的建立者），指出当前AI科学家的核心挑战不再是单个科学组件的自动化，而是系统的集成，即如何将神经学习、逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、模拟、实验设计、机器人技术和正式科学记录相结合。论文还讨论了AI科学家的潜在影响（更快、更便宜、更系统化、更可复现的科学）以及诺贝图灵挑战（2050年前实现自动化诺贝尔级发现的目标）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型、自主代理和实验室机器人技术的快速发展，构建远比亚当和伊芙更通用的AI科学家系统已成为可能。论文指出核心问题已从个别组件能否自动化转变为如何实现系统集成，旨在推动AI科学家领域的进展并设定未来发展方向。

Method: 这是一篇综述性论文，通过回顾历史上两个代表性的AI科学家系统（Adam和Eve），分析当前技术能力（基础模型、自主代理、机器人技术），识别核心瓶颈（组件集成问题），并结合诺贝图灵挑战设定未来目标。

Result: 论文识别出AI科学家发展的核心问题在于集成而非自动化；当前进展超出预期，诺贝图灵挑战（2050年实现诺贝尔级自动化科学发现）的进展提前于计划。

Conclusion: AI科学家有潜力改变科学——使其更快、更便宜、更系统化、更可复现，并能研究人类难以独立处理的复杂系统。若能成功，将催生新的科学形态并改变世界。

Abstract: We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.
  Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern self-driving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.
  The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.
  AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems.
  The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

</details>


### [191] [Knowing When to Stop: Bayesian Optimal Stopping for LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14425)
*Toby D. Pilditch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出optstop，一种基于精度的自适应停止框架，将LLM评估视为序列测量问题，在不确定性高的地方继续采样、估计精确或稳定时停止，在200项10轮评估中可节省57%-97%的计划试验，且结论与完整运行等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估通常使用固定采样预算，对每个测试项进行相同次数的采样，即使在估计已足够精确后仍继续采样，造成计算资源的浪费。

Method: 基于分层贝叶斯推理构建自适应停止框架，支持二值、有序和连续结果，保持所有基准项可采样且无需校准题库；可在线或回溯运行，并包含安全机制：当测量性能接近零时更谨慎采样（因罕见成功至关重要）。

Result: 在200项10轮的示例评估中，optstop在九个验证设置下消除了57%-97%的计划试验，且总体结论与完整运行等价。

Conclusion: LLM评估计算可按不确定性而非固定重复次数来分配，节省幅度取决于评估设计。

Abstract: LLM evaluations often use fixed sampling budgets, testing every item the same number of times even after estimates are precise. We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [192] [The Dynamics of Intelligence Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14426)
*Toby Ord*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI自助研发（AI用于AI研发）反馈循环可能引发智能爆炸的数学条件，指出奇异增长（垂直渐近线）比以往经济学启发模型更难实现，并识别出一类被忽视的超指数但非奇异增长速率，强调"代际时间"是决定智能爆炸行为的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI被越来越多地用于辅助AI研发，这种反馈循环在特定条件下可能产生智能爆炸。作者希望从数学上理解最爆炸性情境的动态机制，特别是审视已有经济学启发模型的假设是否成立，并揭示被忽视的关键参数。

Method: 作者对智能爆炸反馈循环进行数学建模分析，考察增长速率的不同类别（多项式、指数、超指数、奇异增长），重点分析垂直渐近线（奇异点）存在的条件，并将"代际时间"（完成一轮反馈循环所需时间）作为独立参数纳入分析框架。

Result: 研究发现：（1）奇异增长（趋向垂直渐近线）远比以往经济学启发模型所暗示的更难以实现；（2）存在一类重要的增长速率——快于指数增长但不产生垂直渐近线——此前被学界忽视；（3）代际时间是一个被忽视的关键参数，它对智能爆炸的行为起决定性作用，除非代际时间迅速趋近于零，否则不可能实现奇异增长。

Conclusion: 智能爆炸的数学条件比以往认识更严苛。奇异增长需要代际时间急剧缩短至接近零，且非奇异的超指数增长构成了一个被忽视的重要中间类别。这一发现对AI安全与治理策略具有重要含义——需要重新审视对AI加速发展情景的风险评估框架。

Abstract: AI is increasingly being used to help with AI R&D. Under certain conditions this feedback loop might be able to produce an intelligence explosion, with rapidly escalating AI capabilities. I explore the mathematics of the most explosive possibilities, with an eye to understanding what drives the dynamics. I show that singular growth (towards a vertical asymptote) is harder to achieve than would be expected from recent economics-inspired modelling, and that there is an important but neglected class of growth rates that are faster than exponential but don't lead to a vertical asymptote. I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [193] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE-Bench，一个基于模拟器的物理适应基准，包含144个源到目标的环境适应对，横跨六个物理领域，用于评估自主演化智能体在环境条件变化后的恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主演化智能体评估通常在固定执行条件下进行优化，未测试环境条件变化后的恢复能力，这限制了对其真实适应能力的理解。

Method: 构建144个源到目标适应对，覆盖六个物理领域；每个对将源环境与突变的目标环境配对，具有相同目标和接口；源上成功的代码驱动设计在目标中失败，智能体需在有限的尝试预算内利用诊断沙盒反馈迭代调整设计。

Result: 对比了来自四个范式的十种自主演化方法；基准远未饱和：Reflexion+Qwen3-14B仅成功35.9%的全基准配对，GPT-5.5在完整预算下解决了66.7%的静力学子集；模拟器接地反思比未经验证的自我修订更可靠，记忆将智能体锚定在早期设计上，广泛的树搜索探索但不收敛；即使揭示精确的物理变化也不提高性能上限。

Conclusion: 模拟器接地的反思比未经验证的自我修订更可靠，机制重新设计而非参数推断是核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [194] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wyvern是一个多智能体框架，用于自动生成具有信息接地（grounded）的多模态技术报告，支持文、图、表与引用一体化输出，并通过声明自动修订机制增强内容接地性。实验表明其在图表信息性、报告有用性及引用召回率/精确率方面均优于多个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI驱动创新时代知识增长加速，难以追赶；生成模型虽广泛用于内容合成，但常缺乏信息接地（information grounding），导致报告内容缺乏可靠的引用支撑。

Method: 提出Wyvern多智能体框架，支持图像、表格、文本与参考文献统一集成的多模态技术报告自动生成；特别设计声明自动修订（claims auto-revision）阶段以强化内容的接地性。

Result: 人工评估显示：87%的情况下Wyvern生成的图表信息性优于近期基线；在63%至100%的实例中其报告比三种替代方法更有用。自动评估显示，相对基线，Wyvern的引用召回率提升达2.3倍，引用精确率提升达1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern通过多智能体协作与声明自动修订机制，有效提升了技术报告生成的多模态丰富性和内容接地性，在报告质量与引用准确性方面均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [195] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SheetCompass，一个图引导且内存驱动的Agent框架，用于电子表格推理与自动化。该框架显式建模工作表内外的结构关系，并在记忆中维护任务相关信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM处理电子表格的方法通常将多维结构扁平化为顺序字符串，丢失了表内边界和表间语义，导致LLM无法利用人类专家在检查电子表格时自然依赖的全局空间上下文。

Method: SheetCompass是一个图引导且内存驱动的Agent框架，显式建模工作簿内外的结构关系（包括跨表关联、列依赖和空间布局），并在内存中维护任务相关信息以支持Agent推理。

Result: 论文摘要未提供具体实验结果，但提出该框架旨在提升LLM在复杂工作簿上的推理与自动化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模电子表格的多维结构并利用内存驱动机制，SheetCompass使Agent能够更有效地在复杂工作簿上推理，弥补现有扁平化方法的不足。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [196] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种位移感知双编码器迁移框架用于PM2.5短时预测，通过保留目标域特定表示学习并结合源域知识，解决了目标域数据有限且源/目标域统计特性差异时的负迁移问题。在美国10个站点的数据上预训练源编码器，并在台湾77个站点的两年数据上评估，最终模型MSE=21.6575，MAE=3.1383，R²=0.8739。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时PM2.5预测面临双重挑战：目标域观测数据有限，且源域与目标域的统计特性存在差异。仅用本地数据训练的模型难以捕捉复杂的时间动态，而直接迁移学习可能导致负迁移。因此需要有选择性地利用源域知识并保留目标域特异性。

Method: 提出位移感知双编码器迁移框架（shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework），包含两个分支：一个是从美国10个站点预训练的源编码器（承载源域知识），另一个是目标域特异性表示学习分支。通过消融实验评估了冻结源编码器与允许源编码器适应两种策略，并使用SHAP分析进行可解释性评估。

Result: 冻结源编码器的双编码器模型在四个主要基线上表现最佳（MSE=21.8960，MAE=3.1597，R²=0.8725），相比TL-v1提升约7.1%的MSE，相比TL-v2提升4.1%。消融分析表明去除台湾特异性分支导致性能下降最大。允许源编码器适应产生了最优结果（MSE=21.6575，MAE=3.1383，R²=0.8739）。SHAP分析显示预测主要由近期PM2.5观测值和与污染物传输/扩散相关的气象变量驱动。

Conclusion: 源域知识仅在保留目标域特定信息且允许迁移表示在目标监督下适应的条件下才能发挥最大效用。该框架有效避免了负迁移，同时充分利用了跨域知识。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [197] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"测试时世界模型推理"（Twin）系统，让前沿编码智能体为持续学习任务（如ARC-AGI-3游戏）编写可执行的世界模型。Twin通过模拟和交互自动重建游戏规则与目标，并在"孪生世界模型"中做回放验证。该系统在183个关卡中清除179个（97.8%），其中158个关卡（88.3%）的效率超过人类，将基础模型得分从7.8%提升至93.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要为每个任务手工设计定制世界模型，无法在不知道游戏规则和目标的情况下自动适应新任务。作者希望探索能否让编码智能体仅通过仿真和交互来自动构建可执行的世界模型，从而在全新的、规则隐藏的游戏中完成任务，挑战"构建可用世界模型是难点"的普遍假设。

Method: Twin系统的核心流程包括：(1) 前沿编码智能体根据已观察到的游戏交互编写一个可执行的世界模型程序；(2) 每次执行动作前，系统强制要求该程序能复现所有已观测到的游戏状态转移，否则以预测与实际的差异作为反例来修复世界模型；(3) 系统在多网格游戏上使用强归纳偏置，从模拟和交互中恢复真实的转移函数与目标；(4) 目标推断先于任何奖励信号完成，若推理失败则通过搜索自动发现目标。

Result: Twin清除了183个关卡中的179个（97.8%），在179个清除关卡中有158个（88.3%）比人类更高效；在清除的关卡中，156个（87.2%）在获得任何奖励之前就推断出了目标。直接运行基础模型得分仅7.8%，使用现成验证框架提升到61.1%，而Twin将同一基础模型提升到93.3%，清除25个游戏中的23个。

Conclusion: 构建一个可用的世界模型比预期的要简单得多，真正困难的问题在于推断正确的目标。Twin验证了通过自动生成的孪生世界模型进行回放验证的反例驱动修复范式，能够在大幅超越人类的效率和准确率上完成持续学习任务。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [198] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过两项实验（N=809）在三个部署场景（AI肾分配、AI模拟缺席员工、AI生成逝者形象）中系统考察了道德AI elicitation流程中的三个关键选择——特征范围设定、选民抽样和问题措辞——如何影响聚合后的道德偏好，发现每个阶段都会显著改变结果，表明仅靠聚合投票无法实现公平透明的AI对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在越来越多的道德决策场景中发挥作用，道德偏好引导（moral preference elicitation）成为主流应对方案之一。然而，在该流程中开发者在投票前需要做出的三个关键选择（特征范围、选民抽样、问题措辞）常被视为技术细节而被忽视，缺乏系统性评估其对结果的影响，这可能导致AI对齐不公平或不透明。

Method: 研究分两个阶段共招募809名参与者，覆盖三个部署场景（AI肾分配、AI模拟缺席员工、生成式AI描绘逝者）。在每个场景中系统操纵三个流程变量：特征范围设定（观察哪些道德相关特征被纳入投票）、选民抽样（比较不同政治意识形态选民的偏好差异）、以及问题措辞（测试不同表述方式对偏好和道德基础关联的影响）。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）道德相关特征在不同部署场景间存在显著迁移差异，特征模式不能跨领域泛化；（2）约三分之一的特征上偏好因政治意识形态而异，且部分差异方向相反，选民池的意识形态构成会影响聚合偏好；（3）问题措辞可将意识形态差距扩大或缩小多达一个完整量表点，且措辞条件会改变道德基础与参与者判断之间的关联方式。

Conclusion: 投票式对齐无法仅通过聚合本身实现公平或透明的AI。道德AI elicitation流程的每个阶段（特征范围、选民抽样、问题措辞）都应被审计并公开披露，开发者不应将这些选择视为中立的技术细节，而应承认其固有的规范性本质。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


### [199] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用大语言模型的应用中"会话交接"（session handover）的方法论与理论特性。将交接形式化为任务相关上下文学习（ICL）状态的转移，区分为早期材料的精确恢复与目标分布的保持，并在外生性条件下用预测等价性刻画最粗的确定性充分交接及其固定长度比特需求。作者提出将决策与约束精确存储、对重复证据使用任务论证统计量、并保留统计量无法保留效应的原始观测的三部分记录方案，并在高斯线性回归和非参数回归中给出精确的有限维交接与记忆-误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型应用中，当上下文达到输入限制、应用重启或任务转交给其他代理时，任务需要在新会话中继续，但此前会话中的信息如何选择性地传递缺乏理论基础。作者希望回答"交接必须保留什么"以及"记忆需求如何依赖后续任务"这两个核心问题。

Method: 作者将交接形式化为任务相关ICL状态的转移，区分精确恢复与分布保持，引入外生性条件，用预测等价性刻画最粗确定性充分交接并推导固定长度比特要求。提出三部分记录方案：（1）精确存储决策与约束，（2）使用任务论证统计量处理重复证据，（3）保留统计量无法保留效应的原始观测。在Gaussian线性回归中推导精确有限维交接与有限比特扰动界，在非参数回归中建立记忆与平方预测误差之间的上下界。

Result: 给出了交接的理论框架：在外生性条件下预测等价性可以刻画确定性充分交接的最粗粒度，并给出固定长度比特的下界要求。分析隔离了记忆约束、写入者（writer）和续写程序（continuation procedure）对交接的影响，量化了在真实下游查询未知前写入的成本。高斯线性回归有精确的有限维交接，非参数回归给出记忆与预测误差的上下界关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM应用中的会话交接提供了理论基准和方法指导，阐明了交接必须保留什么信息、记忆需求如何随续写任务变化，以及如何设计三部分记录来平衡精确性与效率。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [What preferences can - and cannot - predict in multi-agent online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13810)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了博弈中基于序数/偏好的解概念与博弈动力学长期行为之间的关联，探讨博弈的偏好图（组合数据）在多大程度上决定了无遗憾学习动力学（如FTRL）的结果。作者证明了动态稳定集必须在偏好意义下稳定（封闭于有利偏离），并考察了相反的蕴含方向：偏好能否决定学习动力学的长期行为。作者证明在子博弈情形下偏好可以刻画渐近稳定性，但一般情形下该等价关系不成立（构造了三人博弈反例），最终通过"聚合偏离下的韧性"这一易检验的收益条件弥合了差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是厘清博弈论中两个层面的关系：(1) 基于偏好/序数的静态解概念（偏好图、偏好稳定集）；(2) 基于收益/动力学的动态解概念（无遗憾学习如FTRL的长期行为）。作者试图回答偏好图这一组合数据在多大程度上决定了动力学（尤其是不依赖具体收益数值的序数性质）的长期结果。这一问题的意义在于：若偏好足以决定动力学行为，则无需恢复具体收益数值即可预测学习结果。

Method: 作者采用了双方向的分析框架：(1) 正向：证明每个动态稳定集的骨架（其包含的纯策略组合集）必然偏好稳定，即封闭于有利偏离；(2) 逆向：先证明在子博弈（通过限制玩家动作集获得的纯策略组合子集）情形下，偏好可以刻画渐近稳定性；接着构造一个三人博弈反例，说明一般情形下偏好稳定并不意味着动态稳定（其跨度动态不稳定）；最后引入"聚合偏离下的韧性"这一基于收益的易检验条件，证明它可以保证任意纯策略跨度集的渐近稳定性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 动态稳定集的骨架必然是偏好稳定的；(2) 在子博弈情形下，偏好能够刻画渐近稳定性（等价关系成立）；(3) 一般情形下该等价关系坍塌——构造了三人博弈，其偏好稳定集的跨度动态不稳定；(4) 引入聚合偏离韧性条件，该条件保证了任意纯策略跨度集的渐近稳定性，从而弥合了偏好稳定性与动态稳定性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：偏好图（序数数据）对无遗憾学习动力学的长期行为具有部分但有限的解释力。在子博弈等受限情形下偏好足以刻画动态稳定性，但一般而言偏好数据不足以作为动态稳定性的判据，需要补充基于收益的"聚合偏离韧性"条件来保证渐近稳定性。这一结果表明，在序数偏好与动态稳定性之间，存在一个本质性的边界。

Abstract: We examine the interplay between ordinal, preference-based solution concepts in games and the long-run behavior of game dynamics, asking in particular to what extent the combinatorial data of a game -- its preference graph -- determine the outcomes of no-regret learning dynamics -- such as follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL). In one direction, we show that the skeleton of every dynamically stable set (i.e. the set of pure profiles it contains) must also be preferentially stable, that is, it must be closed under profitable deviations. We then ask the converse question: when do preferences determine the long-run behavior of the players' learning dynamics? We begin by showing that preferences characterize asymptotic stability in the case of subgames -- i.e. subsets of pure profiles obtained by restricting players' action sets. Beyond this case however, the equivalence between dynamic and preferential stability collapses: concretely, we construct a three-player game with a preferentially stable set whose span is dynamically unstable, showing in this way that preferences do not suffice as a criterion of dynamic stability. We then bridge this gap via the notion of resilience under aggregate deviations, an easy-to-check payoff-based condition that guarantees asymptotic stability of arbitrary spans of pure strategies.

</details>


### [201] [Equilibrium Pricing in Oligopolistic Data Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14018)
*Bhaskar Ray Chaudhury,Jugal Garg,Eklavya Sharma,Jiaxin Song*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究预算约束买家（如机器学习公司购买数据提升模型精度）与战略数据卖家之间的寡头数据市场均衡定价问题。研究发现数据非竞争性从根本上改变了定价博弈的性质：纯纳什均衡不一定存在，甚至1.363近似均衡在统一定价下也可能不存在。为此，作者提出分段线性凸定价函数以实现常数因子内的近似稳定性，并验证了2近似纳什均衡上界在实际中优于最坏情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据市场的非竞争性（非排他性）使得传统竞争均衡定价结果不再适用，需要重新审视标准纳什均衡的存在性。同时，现实中的数据买家（如ML公司）通常有预算约束，这进一步复杂化了定价博弈的分析。

Method: 将数据定价建模为卖家间的定价博弈，从理论上分析纯纳什均衡的存在性条件，证明统一定价下精确NE可能不存在且1.363近似NE也可能缺失；随后引入超越统一定价的方案——分段线性凸定价函数，证明存在2近似NE（对抗统一价格偏离）；最后通过模拟验证收敛速度和经验近似保证。

Result: 1) 数据非竞争性使得精确NE不一定存在；2) 统一定价下1.363近似NE也可能不存在；3) 分段线性凸定价函数可保证2近似NE（对统一价格偏离而言）；4) 模拟显示实际收敛速度较快且经验近似因子优于最坏情况上界2。

Conclusion: 数据市场的非竞争性本质要求超越传统均衡概念和定价机制设计。放宽定价机制（从统一价格到凸分段线性定价）是实现稳定均衡的可行途径，但最坏情况与经验表现之间的差距暗示存在更紧的理论界或更优的定价方案有待探索。

Abstract: We study equilibrium pricing in oligopolistic data markets with budget-constrained buyers (e.g., machine learning companies purchasing data to improve model accuracy) and strategic data sellers. Sellers compete by setting prices for their datasets, giving rise to a pricing game whose pure Nash equilibria correspond to equilibrium prices. While equilibrium prices are guaranteed for rivalrous goods via competitive equilibrium, we show that the non-rivalry of data fundamentally alters this picture: an exact Nash equilibrium (NE) need not exist, and in fact, 1.363-approximate NE may also not exist under uniform pricing. We therefore investigate relaxed equilibrium notions. Allowing sellers to use beyond-uniform pricing---specifically, piecewise-linear convex pricing functions---guarantees approximate stability within a constant factor: there exists a pricing profile in which no seller can improve revenue by a factor of two by deviating to any uniform price (a 2-approximate NE against uniform deviations). Finally, our simulations demonstrate fast convergence and empirical approximation guarantees that outperform the worst-case bound of 2.

</details>


### [202] [Optimal Pricing and Charging Strategy Design for Non-cooperative Battery Swapping Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14167)
*Huanyu Yan,Chenxi Sun,Huanxin Liao,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究由同一聚合器协调的多个竞争性电池换电站（BSS）的最优定价与充电策略问题，提出一个两阶段层级博弈模型，并严格证明子博弈完美纳什均衡（SPNE）的存在性与唯一性，基于该均衡设计了最大化收益的定价与充电策略及预测误差处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着越来越多的主体参与建设电池换电站（BSSs），在竞争性市场中非合作BSS如何最大化自身利润的问题需要深入研究。现有研究在多BSS竞争场景下的定价与充电协同优化方面存在空白。

Method: 提出两阶段层级博弈模型：第一阶段BSS在日前市场确定换电价格，第二阶段在实时市场确定最优充电策略。通过数学方法证明了SPNE的存在性与唯一性，并基于唯一SPNE提出最优定价与充电策略，同时设计了预测误差处理方法以应对换电需求的意外波动。

Result: 基于中国西安真实数据的12-BSS系统仿真表明，所提出的定价与充电策略使单个BSS利润至少提升18.1%，且最优充电策略天然实现了电网的削峰填谷效果。

Conclusion: 该研究为竞争性环境下由同一聚合器协调的BSS提供了理论最优的定价与充电策略，SPNE的唯一性保证了策略在竞争环境中的最优性，仿真验证了策略的有效性以及对电网削峰填谷的积极作用。

Abstract: Battery swapping is a rapid way to recharge electric vehicles (EVs). As more and more entities are involved in building Battery Swapping Stations (BSSs), how non-cooperative BSSs maximize their profit in a competitive market needs further investigation. In this paper, we focus on a practical scenario where competitive BSSs are coordinated by the same aggregator. To study the optimal pricing and battery charging, we formulate a hierarchical game-theoretic model, where BSSs determine the swapping price in the day-ahead market in the first stage, and then determine the optimal battery charging strategy in the real-time market in the second stage. We rigorously prove the existence and uniqueness of the Subgame Perfect Nash Equilibrium (SPNE). In particular, the uniqueness property provides theoretical support that the strategy under equilibrium is optimal in the competitive environment. Based on the unique SPNE, we propose an optimal pricing and charging strategy for each BSS to maximize profit in the competitive market. A prediction error handling method is also proposed to deal with unexpected fluctuations in swapping demand. Our simulation with a 12-BSS system based on real-life data from Xi'an, China shows that our pricing and charging strategy increases the individual BSS profit by at least 18.1\%, while the optimal charging strategy naturally achieves peak shaving for the power grid.

</details>


### [203] [Individual Rationality in Constrained Hedonic Games: Friends, Enemies, and Neutrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14461)
*Šimon Schierrreich,Ildikó Schlotter*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究由朋友、敌人和中性关系诱导的hedonic博弈中的约束联盟形成问题。在朋友导向和敌人导向两种偏好细化下，要求恰好k个非空联盟，每个联盟规模满足给定的上下界且个体理性。研究发现敌人导向偏好下问题简化为带规模约束的图着色问题，复杂度随图着色而定；而朋友导向偏好下复杂度由敌意结构而非友谊结构主导，并受规模约束严格程度及关系对称性影响，存在对称情形可解但非对称情形难解的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在个体理性和规模约束（恰好k个联盟、每个联盟有上下界）下，朋友/敌人/中立关系的约束联盟形成问题是否可解。虽然IR单独满足是平凡的，但引入规模约束后计算复杂性变得困难，需要对两种偏好模型进行全面刻画。

Method: 采用经典复杂度与参数化复杂度两种框架，分别分析敌人导向和朋友导向偏好下的决策问题。敌人导向情形归约为带规模约束的图着色；朋友导向情形则按敌意结构、规模约束严格程度（严格/宽松）以及关系的对称性（对称/非对称）进行系统分类，刻画复杂性边界。

Result: 敌人导向偏好下问题等价于规模约束的图着色问题；朋友导向偏好下复杂性由敌意结构主导，且受规模要求和关系对称性调制——几个实例在对称设定下可解，非对称设定下不可解。论文提供了完整的复杂度边界图景，明确了哪些朋友/敌人结构属性导致困难性。

Conclusion: 两种偏好模型在约束联盟形成中呈现出截然不同的复杂性图景：敌人导向的核心是图着色，而朋友导向的困难性根源在于敌意结构，且对称性差异和规模约束的严格性共同决定了可计算性的边界。该结果为理解理性联盟形成的计算本质提供了完整的理论刻画。

Abstract: We study constrained coalition formation in games induced by friends, enemies, and neutrals, under the two standard refinements of additively separable preferences: friend-oriented and enemy-oriented. We ask for partitions that are individually rational (IR), while additionally requiring exactly $k$ non-empty coalitions, each satisfying a prescribed lower and upper bound on its size. Although IR alone is trivial to satisfy for any hedonic game, the size constraints make it computationally intractable to decide whether a feasible partition exists.
  The two models tell strikingly different stories. Under enemy-oriented preferences, the problem collapses to size-constrained graph coloring, and its complexity follows accordingly. Under friend-oriented preferences, however, the picture is far more intricate, and is governed by the enmity structure rather than the friendships. The complexity is further shaped by two factors: how strict the imposed size requirements are, and whether relationships are symmetric or asymmetric, with several cases turning out tractable in the symmetric setting but intractable once asymmetry is allowed. Charting this boundary in terms of both classical and parameterized complexity, we provide a complete understanding of which properties of the friend/enemy structure are responsible for hardness.

</details>


### [204] [Ex-ante versus Ex-post: Egalitarian Facility Location Mechanism Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14499)
*Zohar Barak,Inbal Talgam-Cohen*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究设施选址机制设计问题中的随机策略预期机制。针对均等化成本目标，作者发现：在低维空间中，随机化机制可以打破确定性独裁机制的2倍近似比下界（一维可达最优1，二维通过"随机旋转角"机制达到1.598），并证明了二维空间中事前与事后评估之间存在严格分离；但在高维空间中，任何策略预期机制都无法超越确定性独裁机制的o_d(1)，并证明RRCWM机制在高维均等化目标下也是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设施选址机制设计中，均等化成本的确定性最优近似比为2（由独裁机制实现）。先前随机化机制的研究聚焦于事后评估（期望最大成本），但作者指出事前评估（最大期望成本）与策略预期机制的内在性质更为一致，值得系统研究随机化机制在事前评估下的能力以及对事后评估的分离关系。

Method: (1) 在R^1中设计了简单策略预期机制达到事前最优近似比1；(2) 在R^2中设计了"Random Rotated Corner"机制，证明其事前近似比至多为1.598；(3) 对R^2事后目标给出1.605的下界，实现严格分离；(4) 在高维空间证明一般不可能性结果；(5) 分析RRCWM机制在高维均等化目标下的事前和事后近似比均为2。

Result: (1) R^1：事前近似比可达最优值1；(2) R^2：Random Rotated Corner机制事前近似比≤1.598，打破确定性下界2；事后下界1.605，实现事前与事后评估的严格分离；(3) R^d (d≫1)：无任何策略预期机制能优于确定性独裁机制超过o_d(1)；(4) RRCWM对所有d≥1在均等化目标下的事前和事后近似比均为2。

Conclusion: 随机化机制的力量具有维度依赖性：在低维空间中通过事前评估可以大幅改善均等化公平性（一维最优、二维突破确定性下界），但高维空间中随机化策略预期机制在公平性目标下无法提供任何实质性改进。同时，RRCWM在高维均等化目标下为最优机制。

Abstract: We study the facility location mechanism design problem where $n$ strategic agents report locations in Euclidean space and the mechanism outputs a single facility location. Each agent's cost is its distance from the facility, and our objective is to minimize the egalitarian cost, i.e., the maximum agent cost, in a strategyproof way.
  The optimal deterministic approximation ratio is $2$, achieved by any dictator mechanism. We study the power of randomized strategyproof-in-expectation mechanisms. Prior work has focused on ex-post evaluation, defined as the expected maximum agent cost. We instead study ex-ante evaluation, defined as the maximum expected agent cost, which is naturally aligned with strategyproofness in expectation.
  We establish the following results:
  (1) Low dimensions: Strict ex-ante vs. ex-post separation. In $\mathbb{R}$, we give a simple strategyproof mechanism achieving the optimal ex-ante approximation ratio of $1$. In $\mathbb{R}^2$, we design the "Random Rotated Corner" mechanism, with ex-ante approximation ratio at most $1.598$, breaking the deterministic barrier. For the ex-post objective, we prove a lower bound of $1.605$, yielding a strict separation in $\mathbb{R}^2$.
  (2) High dimensions: Impossibility. In $\mathbb{R}^d$ for $d \gg 1$, we show that no strategyproof-in-expectation mechanism improves on the deterministic dictator mechanism beyond $o_d(1)$. Thus neither ex-post nor ex-ante evaluation yields improved fairness guarantees in high dimensions. An implication is that the "Random Rotation Coordinate-Wise Median" (RRCWM), currently the best known mechanism for the utilitarian objective, is also best possible for the egalitarian objective in high dimension: we show it achieves an approximation ratio of $2$ for both ex-post and ex-ante objectives in $\mathbb{R}^d$ for every $d \ge 1$.

</details>


### [205] [Forging Self-Funded Marketplaces among Strategic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14548)
*Yuan Deng,Vasilis Gkatzelis,Xizhi Tan,Grigoris Velegkas,Song Zuo*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何设计机制以激励策略性主体形成自筹资金的市场。模型允许代理的成本c_i未知且可大于或小于其收入r_i，目标是在满足预算收支平衡和个体理性约束下最大化总价值。文中证明没有任何真实拍卖能对第一最佳基准实现有界逼近，并给出了对数近似的序贯拍卖；同时引入最大化最小份额（MMS）基准，提出能实现常数近似的拍卖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预算可行机制设计问题假设存在一个预定的外部预算B（即sum p_i <= B），但现实中许多市场（如去中心化平台、自组织市场）需要由参与者自筹资金，即收入来源来自参与者自身产生的收益（sum p_i <= sum x_i * r_i）。此外，每个代理的成本c_i对机制设计者是未知的，且可能高于或低于其收入，这使得问题在信息不对称和预算约束的双重挑战下更加困难。

Method: 本文首先对第一最佳基准进行不可逼近性分析：证明在一般情形下不存在有界近似的诚实拍卖，即使在受限场景下也只能达到对数近似的下界。然后提出一类序贯拍卖（sequential auctions），其子博弈完美均衡（SPE）能保证第一最佳基准的对数近似。最后引入一个替代性基准——最大化最小份额（MMS），以更好地反映市场的厚度（即参与者数量/市场密度带来的价值），并设计一个拍卖，其子博弈完美均衡对该基准能达到常数近似。

Result: （1）对第一最佳基准：无任何诚实拍卖能达到有界近似；即使受限设置下，下界为对数近似。（2）所提出的序贯拍卖在子博弈完美均衡下对第一最佳基准达到对数近似。（3）基于MMS基准，设计的拍卖在子博弈完美均衡下达到常数近似。

Conclusion: 自行筹资市场的机制设计本质上比传统的预算可行机制设计更难（第一最佳基准下存在对数不可逼近下界），但通过引入更符合市场厚度特征的MMS基准，可以设计出在均衡下实现常数近似的有效拍卖，为自筹资金市场中的机制设计提供了理论可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce the problem of designing mechanisms that incentivize strategic agents to form self-funded marketplaces. In our model, if agent $i$ exerts effort $x_i\in [0,1]$, they incur a cost of $x_i\cdot c_i$ (where $c_i$ is unknown to the mechanism designer) and they generate revenue $x_i\cdot r_i$; crucially, $c_i$ can be greater or smaller than $r_i$. Each effort profile $\mathbf{x}$ yields value $v(\mathbf{x})$ and the objective is to choose an effort vector that maximizes the value while ensuring that every agent $i$ receives a payment $p_i\geq x_i\cdot c_i$ and that $\mathbf{x}$ is budget-balanced, i.e., $\sum_{i} p_i \leq \sum_{i} x_i\cdot r_i$. This problem generalizes the well-studied budget-feasible mechanism design problem, where the requirement is that $\sum_{i} p_i \leq B$ for some predetermined budget $B$.
  To evaluate the performance of such mechanisms, we first consider the first-best benchmark (the optimal value achievable in the absence of any private information) and show that no truthful auction can achieve a bounded approximation of this benchmark. Also, even in restricted settings, no auction can achieve better than a logarithmic approximation. We complement these results by proposing a class of sequential auctions whose subgame perfect equilibria guarantee a logarithmic approximation of this benchmark. We then introduce an alternative benchmark, the maximin share (MMS), that better captures the thickness of the market and we provide an auction whose subgame perfect equilibria achieve a constant approximation of this benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [AlphaSeek: Trajectory-Level Self-Iterative Factor Mining Framework for Multi-Source Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13913)
*Qilu Zhu,Zijun Lu,Jianmin Zhu,Ning Chen,Shuo Yin,Simon Fong*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: AlphaSeek是一个端到端的量化因子挖掘框架，整合自动方向发现、轨迹级因子演化挖掘和自我迭代的投资组合优化，在CSI300上取得了最优策略级表现（ARR 8.28%、IR 1.29、MDD 6.28%），并在CSI500上展现出良好的零样本跨市场迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的量化因子挖掘方法存在多个局限：方向设计过于主观、多源信息整合不够及时充分、存在语义漂移、因子冗余，以及缺乏从因子发现到组合回测的端到端反馈闭环。这些不足限制了因子挖掘的质量和实际投资效果。

Method: AlphaSeek包含三个核心模块：（1）自动方向发现：收集并总结多源金融信息以识别有前景的挖掘方向；（2）轨迹级因子演化挖掘：将优化单元从单个因子表达式扩展为涵盖假设生成、因子构建、验证、回测和反馈的完整研究轨迹，并引入并行方向扩展、变异和交叉三种演化算子以提升搜索多样性、精炼质量和因子鲁棒性；（3）自我迭代因子组合：在冗余感知约束下，让新发现的因子与现有SOTA因子库交互，构建自我迭代的因子组合。

Result: 在CSI300上的实验表明，AlphaSeek取得整体最优的策略级表现：ARR为8.28%、IR为1.29、MDD为6.28%，同时因子预测指标IC为0.0454，保持竞争力。此外，在CSI300上挖掘的因子在CSI500上也取得了强于其他模型的时间序列收益表现，展示了零样本情境下的跨市场迁移潜力。

Conclusion: AlphaSeek通过端到端设计和轨迹级演化机制，有效解决了现有LLM驱动因子挖掘中的关键痛点，验证了其在实际策略表现和跨市场泛化能力上的优越性，为LLM在量化投资中的落地提供了新的范式。

Abstract: With the rapid rise of large language models, LLM-driven quantitative factor mining has become an increasingly active research area. However, existing methods still suffer from subjective direction design, limited integration of up-to-date multi-source information, semantic drift, factor redundancy, and the absence of an end-to-end feedback loop from factor discovery to portfolio backtesting. To address these limitations, we propose AlphaSeek, an end-to-end factor mining framework for quantitative investment that integrates automated direction discovery, trajectory-level factor evolution mining and self-iterative portfolio optimization. AlphaSeek first collects and summarizes multi-source financial information to identify promising mining directions. It then performs trajectory-level factor mining by extending the optimization unit from a single factor expression to a complete research trajectory covering hypothesis generation, factor construction, validation, backtesting, and feedback. Based on this design, we introduce evolution operators - parallel direction expansion, mutation and crossover - to improve search diversity, refinement quality and factor robustness. Finally, AlphaSeek constructs a self-iterative factor portfolio, allowing newly discovered factors to interact with an existing state-of-the-art(SOTA) factor library under redundancy-aware constraints. Experiments on CSI300 show that AlphaSeek achieves the strongest overall strategy-level performance on CSI300 with ARR of 8.28%, IR of 1.29 and MDD of 6.28%, while remaining competitive on factor predictive metrics with IC of 0.0454, while factors mined on CSI300 also achieve strong time-series return performance on CSI500 than other models, suggesting promising cross-market transferability under a zero-shot setting.

</details>


### [207] [MMDynOpt-Agent: Dynamic Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Model Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14026)
*Wenjin Liu,Haoran Luo,Fayuan Ke,Zhenghong Lin,Yue Lu,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出MMDynOpt-Agent，将多模态推理的动态优化建模为马尔可夫决策过程，通过端到端强化学习训练轻量级多模态智能体，与目标MLLM交互实现自适应多轮动态优化提示，并设计包含格式合规、答案正确性和预算意识的奖励机制，在15个公共数据集上取得优异性能且可迁移泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以高效地将多模态输入的视觉线索和问题语义转化为有效的推理条件，限制了多模态大语言模型的推理性能。

Method: 将多模态推理的动态优化建模为马尔可夫决策过程，使用端到端强化学习；轻量级多模态智能体作为决策策略，将目标MLLM作为环境，通过多轮动态优化提示自适应引导推理；设计结合格式合规、答案正确性和预算意识的奖励机制以平衡推理准确性和效率。

Result: 在15个公共数据集上的实验表明，MMDynOpt-Agent取得了强性能并优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MMDynOpt-Agent具有可迁移性和泛化性，支持用单一目标MLLM训练、推理时迁移到其他模型，为多模态推理的动态优化提供了有效且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recently, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong potential in visual understanding and complex reasoning tasks. However, existing methods often struggle to efficiently transform visual cues from multimodal inputs and the semantics of the question into effective reasoning conditions, thereby limiting the reasoning performance of multimodal large language models. To address this challenge, we propose MMDynOpt-Agent, which models the dynamic optimization of multimodal reasoning as a Markov decision process via end-to-end reinforcement learning. Specifically, a lightweight multimodal agent serves as the decision policy and interacts with the target MLLM as the environment, adaptively steering its reasoning through multi-turn dynamic optimization prompts. Furthermore, to reduce the cost of multimodal reasoning, a reward mechanism that combines format compliance, answer correctness, and budget awareness is designed to jointly ensure reasoning accuracy and efficiency. MMDynOpt-Agent is transferable and generalizable, enabling training with one target MLLM and inference-time transfer to others. Experimental results on fifteen public datasets show MMDynOpt-Agent achieves strong performance and outperforms baselines. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/MMDynOpt-Agent.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Forecast Collapse in Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14106)
*Shu Wan,Miles Ma,Hank Zhu,Guangqi Liu,Stephen Wang,Qingsong Wen,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了金融时间序列预测中的"预测坍缩"现象：在预测1,000只美股的小时收益率时，模型输出近乎平坦且股票排序能力差（低横截面相关性），但预测交易量时该现象几乎消失。作者发现该现象与目标可预测性密切相关，源于两个原因：低可预测性限制了校准点预测的幅度，以及逐序列目标函数未能识别跨序列结构。作者提出CalibRank目标函数，在保持校准幅度的同时将横截面相关性提升近3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列评估仅关注逐序列指标（如MSE），忽略了跨序列结构（如股票间的横截面排序），导致下游决策（如投资组合构建）依赖的关键信息未被捕捉。作者观察到预测坍缩现象，旨在理解其成因并提出解决方案。

Method: 通过系统性实验在时序基础模型（TSFMs）、12种深度学习预测模型和97个公开基准配置上调查预测坍缩现象，识别其与目标可预测性的关联并分解出两个成因。基于校准-排序权衡分析，提出CalibRank目标函数，该目标在最小化平方误差（保证校准）与优化横截面相关性（保证排序）之间取得平衡。

Result: 在Finance1K数据集上，CalibRank使横截面相关性提升近3倍，同时将预测幅度保持在接近真实目标值的水平，并在所有测试模型上均改善了横截面相关性。

Conclusion: 传统逐序列时间序列评估指标存在盲区：它们无法揭示跨序列结构的失败。预测坍缩现象以及校准-排序权衡表明，时间序列预测模型需要同时兼顾逐序列校准和跨序列结构，以满足下游决策需求。

Abstract: When forecasting hourly returns for 1,000 US equities, we observe an unexpected phenomenon: predictions become nearly flat and show poor stock ranking, as measured by cross-sectional correlation. We call this forecast collapse. Surprisingly, the phenomenon largely disappears when forecasting trading volume under the same setting. We investigate forecast collapse across time-series foundation models (TSFMs), twelve deep-learning forecasting models, and 97 public benchmark configurations, and find that it is closely tied to target predictability. We identify two distinct reasons behind it: low predictability limits the amplitude of calibrated point forecasts, while per-series objectives leave cross-series structure unidentified. These findings reveal a calibration-ranking tradeoff: optimizing squared error leads to flat predictions, whereas directly optimizing cross-sectional correlation improves ranking but can inflate forecast amplitude by more than an order of magnitude. To address this tradeoff, we introduce CalibRank, a simple objective that balances calibration and ranking. On Finance1K, CalibRank nearly triples cross-sectional correlation while keeping amplitude close to the target, and improves correlation on all tested models. Our results reveal a blind spot in conventional time-series evaluation: per-series metrics can hide failures in cross-series structure needed by downstream decisions.

</details>


### [209] [L-FNO: Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator for Stochastic Event Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13562)
*Songhee Kang,Jihoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lorentzian傅里叶神经算子(L-FNO)，一种结合FNO风格协变量路径、Lorentzian谱核与似然训练目标的随机神经算子，用于条件强度估计和稀有事件建模，在8个合成点过程基准和3个真实数据集上优于回归式和似然式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代运行系统在常规条件下也面临不确定性，稀有、突发且自激事件同时受外生协变量和内生事件动态驱动。标准神经算子通常被训练为回归式函数到函数的映射，而非条件强度估计器，限制了其在稀疏事件场景中的适用性。

Method: L-FNO由三部分构成：(1) FNO风格的协变量路径用于处理外生输入；(2) Lorentzian谱核用于建模历史依赖的激发效应；(3) 基于似然的训练目标用于学习事件强度函数。该方法将谱域记忆结构与点过程似然相结合。

Result: 在8个合成点过程基准和3个涵盖疾病暴发预测与半导体故障/缺陷检测的真实数据集上，L-FNO在事件似然、校准诊断和稀有事件检测方面均优于回归式和似然式神经算子基线。

Conclusion: 结构化谱记忆和基于似然的学习为神经算子模型提供了有效的归纳偏置，使其能够更好地建模随机事件动态。

Abstract: Modern operational systems face uncertainty even in routine conditions, where rare, bursty, and self-exciting events emerge from both exogenous covariates and endogenous event dynamics. Standard neural operators are typically trained as regression-style function-to-function models rather than conditional-intensity estimators, limiting their suitability for sparse event regimes. We introduce the Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator (L-FNO), a stochastic neural operator that combines an FNO-style covariate path, Lorentzian spectral kernels for history-dependent excitation, and a likelihood-based training objective. We evaluate L-FNO on eight synthetic point-process benchmarks and three real-world datasets covering disease outbreak prediction and semiconductor fault or defect detection. L-FNO improves event likelihood, calibration diagnostics, and rare-event detection over regression- and likelihood-based neural operator baselines. These results show that structured spectral memory and likelihood-based learning provide effective inductive biases for neural operator models of stochastic event dynamics.

</details>


### [210] [Don't Claim Benchmark-Oriented Optimization Improves General Coding Capability -- Diverse Evaluation Is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13566)
*Egor Shibaev,Vera Kudrevskaia,Timur Galimzyanov,Mikhail Evtikhiev,Ana Terna,Rastislav Rabatin,Timur Kudashev,Timofey Bryksin,Arina Puchkova,Patrik Bartak,Egor Bogomolov,Sergey Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有编码基准（如SWE-bench和LiveCodeBench）被过度用作"广泛编码能力"的证据，但基准优化导致测评分数与实际泛化能力之间存在意义鸿沟。作者通过自建Django案例评测集发现，针对SWE-bench轨迹进行post-training的模型和检查点在跨任务迁移上表现有限，基准排名经常无法泛化。因此，小规模基准不足以支撑广泛能力声称，社区应采用差异化评估（前沿模型综合评估、研究用多任务套件、窄任务用人在环研究），并建立能力分类体系和可持续的基准维护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数被广泛用作编码能力的证据，但针对特定基准的优化会导致测得的性能仅反映任务特定能力，而非普遍编码能力，造成测评得分与能力声称之间产生"意义鸿沟"（meaning gap）。

Method: 作者构建了基于Django的案例研究基准套件，对在SWE-bench轨迹上post-training的基础模型和检查点进行评估，检验基准排名是否能泛化到新任务，同时考察SWE-bench优化对LivaCodeBench及自建Django任务的迁移效果，并测试针对单个Django模态的微调是否具有跨模态迁移能力。

Result: 基准排名经常无法泛化；post-trained检查点几乎无跨任务迁移；SWE-bench优化在自建Django任务或LiveCodeBench上仅有有限或无增益；单个Django模态的微调同样无法向其他模态迁移。

Conclusion: 小规模基准在基准优化压力下不足以评估多样化模型。社区应采用差异化评估策略，创建编码能力分类体系（capability taxonomy），并持续维护基准而非一次性发布。缺乏可靠的评估标准会使工程师和研究人员在研发和部署决策中依赖不充分的证据。

Abstract: Post-training papers, model cards, and blog posts often treat scores on a small set of coding benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench and LiveCodeBench) as evidence of broad coding capability, both for research artifacts and user-facing systems. We argue that optimization for these benchmarks leads to measuring task-specific performance, creating a meaning gap between measured scores and claims of general coding ability. We examine this gap with a Django-based case study benchmark suite we create.
  Evaluating foundation models and checkpoints post-trained on SWE-bench trajectories, we find that benchmark rankings frequently fail to generalize. Post-trained checkpoints show little cross-task transfer, and SWE-bench optimization yields limited or no gains on our tasks or on LiveCodeBench. Similarly, fine-tuning on individual Django modalities fails to transfer.
  We conclude that a small number of benchmarks is insufficient for evaluating diverse models under benchmark optimization pressure. We encourage the community to use differentiated evaluation - holistic assessment for frontier models, multi-task suites for research, and human-in-the-loop studies for narrow task applications. Finally, we argue for creating a capability taxonomy and sustained benchmark maintenance, rather than one-off benchmark releases. Without reliable evaluation standards, engineers and researchers using LLMs and agents have to rely on insufficient evidence to make research, development, and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [211] [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590)
*Iris Aragón Mladosich,Christophe Croux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了XGBoost的鲁棒性问题，发现其性能会受垂直离群值和杠杆点的影响。为解决这一问题，作者探索了基于M、S和τ估计量的替代损失函数，并提出了MM-XGBoost两阶段方法，在鲁棒性和预测精度之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XGBoost作为广泛使用的预测工具，其标准损失函数（二次损失）对异常值敏感，垂直离群值和杠杆点会显著影响其性能。现有研究缺乏对XGBoost在异常值存在下鲁棒性的系统性分析。

Method: 作者系统研究了XGBoost在垂直离群值和杠杆点存在下的表现，并探索了基于鲁棒回归中M估计量、S估计量和τ估计量的替代损失函数。其中MM-XGBoost采用两阶段程序：第一阶段用鲁棒方法获得初始估计，第二阶段在初始值基础上进行MM估计以同时兼顾鲁棒性和效率。

Result: 实验结果表明，MM-XGBoost（两阶段方法）在鲁棒性与预测精度之间提供了最佳权衡，优于标准的二次损失XGBoost以及其他基于单一鲁棒估计量的变体。

Conclusion: 作者推荐采用MM-XGBoost作为对存在异常值数据场景下的鲁棒XGBoost实现方案，它能够在保持较高预测精度的同时有效抵御垂直离群值和杠杆点的影响。

Abstract: XGBoost is a very popular and powerful method for prediction. It iteratively fits simple decision trees to the residuals of the previous step. An efficient and scalable implementation is available. The standard loss function for XGBoost is the quadratic loss, but a Huber loss can also be used. In this paper, we study the robustness of XGBoost and show that its performance can be affected by vertical outliers and leverage points. To address this, we explore alternative loss functions, based on M-, S-, and τ -estimators from robust regression. Our results indicate that a two-step procedure, referred to as MM-XGBoost, provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [212] [Training-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Model Scales through Activation-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13596)
*Jiahe Fan,Si Chen,Yinghao Hou,Aiyuan Zhang,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Activation-Prune-Merge (APM)框架，实现无需神经单元语义对齐的跨尺度异构模型融合。通过激活引导的剪枝技术，将大规模donor模型裁剪为与小型recipient模型匹配的结构后，以微插值系数注入，在16个基准上将3B模型的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构模型融合旨在结合在任务、初始化、架构或规模上不同的模型，但跨尺度（跨规模）情形下，小模型与大模型之间的架构不匹配严重，传统方法依赖逐神经元语义对齐，难以实现。作者希望探索在无需显式语义对齐的情况下，能否将更强donor模型的有用能力转移至较小的recipient模型。

Method: APM框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 在donor上构建任务条件激活图；(2) 基于激活图选择显著层、隐藏维度、注意力头和MLP神经元，将donor剪枝为与recipient匹配的架构；(3) 使用微插值系数将剪枝得到的donor切片注入原始recipient。该方法将donor视为集中式功能组件的来源，而非要求精确的结构移植。

Result: 在16个覆盖推理、数学、代码生成、指令跟随和分类的基准上，APM将3B recipient的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。具体上，RTE从64.3%提升至82.3%，QNLI从52.3%提升至65.7%，BoolQ从70.8%提升至79.2%。注入比例分析和多阶段融合的后续实验进一步表明激活引导提取提高了可迁移donor切片的质量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当donor的贡献足够集中且被仔细选择时，跨尺度异构融合无需显式语义对齐即可取得成功。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model fusion seeks to combine models that differ in tasks, initializations, architectures, or scales. We study an underexplored cross-scale setting: improving a small recipient language model with a stronger donor despite substantial architectural mismatch. We ask whether useful capabilities can be transferred without explicit neuron-wise semantic alignment. Building on the observation that truncating a large model to a smaller architecture and injecting it with a tiny mixing weight can already improve the recipient, we propose Activation-Prune-Merge (APM), an activation-guided framework for cross-scale fusion. APM constructs task-conditioned activation maps on the donor, selects salient layers, hidden dimensions, attention heads, and MLP neurons to prune it to the recipient architecture, and injects the resulting donor slice into the original recipient using a micro interpolation coefficient. This formulation treats the donor as a source of concentrated functional components rather than requiring precise structural transplantation. Across 16 benchmarks spanning reasoning, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and classification, APM improves the overall average accuracy from 55.5% to 60.6% over the original 3B recipient. RTE accuracy increases from 64.3% to 82.3%, QNLI from 52.3% to 65.7%, and BoolQ from 70.8% to 79.2%. Analyses of injection ratios and sequential multi-stage fusion further suggest that activation-guided extraction improves the quality of the transferable donor slice while preserving the small-ratio fusion regime. These results provide evidence that cross-scale heterogeneous fusion can succeed without explicit semantic alignment when the donor contribution is sufficiently concentrated and carefully selected.

</details>


### [213] [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601)
*John Myron Uy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对比边际不确定性采样与随机采样在干净标签、随机分类噪声和难度依赖噪声下的表现，检验不确定性采样是否因获取更多错误标签或在困难区域错误集中而失效。实验跨越100个配对种子、9种噪声率、多个标注预算和三个公开二分类表格数据集，发现不确定性采样在干净标签下始终提升学习曲线归一化平衡准确率AUC 1.09至1.77个百分点，但其鲁棒性依赖于数据集、预算、噪声结构和评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习通过选择信息量最大的样本来降低标注成本，但最不确定的样本往往也是最难正确标注的。现有研究未明确区分不确定性采样失败的两种机制：是获得了更多损坏的标签，还是困难区域中的错误集中造成了特别大的危害。本文旨在通过对照实验设计解耦这两种效应。

Method: 采用边际不确定性采样与随机采样对照，在三种标签噪声条件（干净标签、随机分类噪声RCN、有界难度依赖噪声）下，使用100个配对种子、9个期望噪声率（0-0.30）、标注预算20-120（干净标签扩展至400），数据集为三个公开的二分类表格数据集。逻辑回归在每个预算下通过交叉验证重新选择正则化参数。设计暴露匹配RCN对照以对齐平均最终获取的污染标签量，另设干净标签扩展实验。

Result: 干净标签下，不确定性采样在所有数据集上提升了归一化平衡准确率学习曲线AUC 1.09至1.77个百分点。难度依赖噪声在Breast Cancer Wisconsin上8个噪声率中的6个比RCN更能削弱这一优势，但在Banknote Authentication和MAGIC Gamma Telescope上无此效应。暴露匹配分析未发现结构化错误位置带来额外惩罚的统一证据。在干净的MAGIC数据上，不确定性采样提高了平衡准确率但降低了平均精确率和固定假阳性率下的真阳性率。

Conclusion: 不确定性采样在标签效率上是有效的，但其表面上的鲁棒性取决于数据集、预算、噪声结构以及评估指标。不存在一个普遍适用的结论——不确定性采样的优势或劣势需要在具体的数据-噪声-指标组合下进行评估。

Abstract: Active learning can reduce labeling cost by selecting informative examples, but the most uncertain examples may also be the hardest to label correctly. This study tests whether uncertainty sampling fails because it acquires more corrupted labels or because errors concentrated in difficult regions are especially harmful. Margin-based uncertainty sampling is compared with random sampling under clean labels, random classification noise (RCN), and bounded difficulty-dependent noise on three public binary tabular datasets. The design uses 100 paired seeds, nine expected noise rates from 0 to 0.30, annotation budgets from 20 to 120, and logistic regression with regularization re-selected by cross-validation at every budget. An exposure-matched RCN control aligns mean final acquired corruption, while a clean-label extension reaches budget 400. Under clean labels, uncertainty sampling improved normalized balanced-accuracy area under the learning curve by 1.09 to 1.77 percentage points on all datasets. Difficulty-dependent noise reduced this advantage more than RCN at six of eight rates on Breast Cancer Wisconsin, but at no tested rate on Banknote Authentication or MAGIC Gamma Telescope. Exposure-matched analyses found no corrected evidence for a universal additional penalty from structured error location. On clean MAGIC data, uncertainty sampling improved balanced accuracy while reducing average precision and true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rates. Thus, uncertainty sampling was label-efficient, but its apparent robustness depended on dataset, budget, noise structure, and evaluation metric.

</details>


### [214] [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628)
*A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于核风险敏感性均p次方准则的随机向量功能连接网络（KRPRVFL）模型，通过用核风险敏感均值p次方（KRP）准则替代传统最小二乘目标函数，并引入协作学习机制和核诱导特征映射，有效解决了传统RVFL网络对噪声标签、离群点和不平衡数据敏感的问题，在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上取得了优于基线模型的准确率、鲁棒性和统计显著性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机向量功能连接（RVFL）网络虽然具有轻量、高效、训练快、泛化能力强的优点，但对噪声标签、离群点和不平衡数据非常敏感，这严重限制了其在真实世界应用中的性能表现。因此，需要一种既能保持RVFL计算效率又能具备鲁棒性的改进模型。

Method: KRPRVFL模型的核心方法是：(1) 用核风险敏感均值p次方（KRP）准则替换标准最小二乘目标函数，自适应降低训练过程中损坏或不可靠样本的影响；(2) 引入协作学习机制，使模型各组件之间实现自适应交互，增强在复杂噪声环境下的鲁棒性；(3) 利用核诱导特征映射捕捉非线性关系，无需显式选择隐藏层，保持模型效率和可扩展性。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上进行了大量实验，KRPRVFL在准确率、鲁棒性和统计显著性方面始终优于基线模型，证明了其作为快速、可扩展且可靠的分类解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: KRPRVFL模型成功地将RVFL的计算效率与KRP准则的鲁棒性相结合，有效解决了噪声标签、离群点和数据不平衡等挑战，为复杂的分类任务提供了一种兼具效率、可扩展性和可靠性的解决方案。

Abstract: Random vector functional link (RVFL) networks are lightweight and fast neural models that offer efficient training and strong generalization through randomized hidden-layer weights and direct input-output connections. However, conventional RVFL models are sensitive to noisy labels, outliers, and imbalanced data, which limits their performance in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power based RVFL (KRPRVFL) model, which integrates the computational efficiency of RVFL with the robustness of the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power (KRP) criterion. By replacing the standard least-squares objective with a KRP-based loss, KRPRVFL adaptively reduces the influence of corrupted or unreliable samples during training, resulting in improved stability and generalization. Additionally, a collaborative learning mechanism is introduced to enable adaptive interaction among model components, further enhancing robustness in complex and noisy environments. The proposed framework also leverages kernel-induced feature mapping to capture nonlinear relationships without requiring explicit hidden-layer selection, maintaining both efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets demonstrate that KRPRVFL consistently outperforms baseline models in terms of accuracy, robustness, and statistical significance, highlighting its effectiveness as a fast, scalable, and reliable solution for challenging classification tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652)
*Haoyi Jia,Sagar Addepalli,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ORCA（Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection）框架，通过两阶段方法解决对撞机物理中事件级异常检测的两个核心问题：异常分数难以解释，以及异常分数与能量标度和物体多重性强相关。ORCA首先利用监督对比学习在多种物理过程上学习嵌入空间，然后在该空间运行标准自编码器生成事件级异常分数。在高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）条件下模拟数据上，ORCA相比基线自编码器在灵敏度的广度和深度上均有显著提升，且对比嵌入使异常样本具有可解释性——通过最大似然模板拟合可归因异常事件并量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对撞机物理中通用事件级异常检测面临两个反复出现的问题：一是异常分数难以解释，二是异常分数与能量标度和物体多重性强烈相关，导致基于粒子的低层级异常信号的检测灵敏度受限。现有方法难以在保持敏感性的同时提供可解释的物理洞察。

Method: ORCA采用两阶段框架：(1) 第一阶段通过监督对比学习在一组多样化的物理过程上学习一个嵌入式表示空间，使已知过程在空间中占据不同区域；(2) 第二阶段在该嵌入空间上运行标准自编码器生成事件级异常分数。此外，通过对嵌入分布进行最大似然模板拟合，可以将异常样本中的事件归因到模板物理过程并给出量化不确定性。

Result: 在模拟的HL-LHC条件下，ORCA相比基线自编码器在对新物理信号的灵敏度（广度与深度）上均取得显著提升。模板拟合能准确恢复注入的信号产额（包括训练中未包含的信号），并能通过已知过程对模板库中缺失的信号进行特征化描述。

Conclusion: ORCA为实现可解释的、基于异常检测的对撞机搜索提供了可行路径，其嵌入几何包含比标准一维输出拟合更高维的物理信息，增强了下游统计分析能力。

Abstract: Generic event-level anomaly detection for collider physics has two recurring problems: anomaly scores are hard to interpret, and they correlate strongly with energy scale and object multiplicity. We present Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection (ORCA), a two-stage framework that first learns an embedding space via supervised contrastive learning across a diverse set of physics processes, then runs a standard autoencoder in that space to generate event-level anomaly scores. On a simulated dataset consistent with conditions at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, ORCA delivers significant gains in both breadth and depth of sensitivity to new physics signals with respect to a baseline autoencoder architecture. Beyond improved sensitivity, the contrastive embedding makes the anomalous sample interpretable: because known processes occupy distinct regions of the space, a maximum-likelihood template fit to the embedding distributions can attribute events in an anomalous sample to template physics processes with quantified uncertainties. We demonstrate that the fit accurately recovers injected signal yields, including for signals excluded from the training of the embedding, and characterizes signals absent from the template library through the known processes they most resemble. These results establish ORCA as a route to interpretable anomaly detection-based searches at colliders, where the embedding geometry carries higher dimensional physics information compared to standard one-dimensional output fits, enhancing downstream statistical analysis.

</details>


### [216] [The Query Knows What to Forget: A Second Erase Direction for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13668)
*Dhruman Gupta,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QED（Query-derived Erase Direction），通过引入从查询派生且与键正交的第二个擦除方向，解决了线性注意力在长上下文中的检索干扰问题，使可用上下文长度约翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力维护固定大小的状态，在长上下文中大量存储项共享该状态，导致项间干扰削弱检索能力。现有的delta-rule模型（如GDN-2）的擦除向量仅从当前token的键派生，但干扰是通过查询测量的，键派生的擦除无法有效触及查询方向的干扰。

Method: 提出QED方法，在原有键派生擦除方向之外，添加一个从查询派生且与键正交的擦除方向。在快速权重视角下，键导向的delta编辑无法改变读取中与键正交的部分，而QED利用这一可编辑部分来取消沿查询测量的旧状态内容。

Result: QED在所有超过训练窗口的长度上均改善了检索性能，在S-NIAH-1任务上使可用上下文长度增加了约一倍。

Conclusion: 从查询而非仅从键派生的擦除方向能更有效地消除线性注意力状态中的干扰，显著提升长上下文检索能力，为delta-rule类模型的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Linear attention keeps a state of fixed size. At long context, many stored items share this state, and interference between them degrades retrieval. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2), like every delta-rule model before it, derives its erase vector from the key of the current token. However, the interference in its reads is measured through the query, and the erase step cannot reach it. We introduce the Query-derived Erase Direction (QED). QED adds a second erase direction derived from the query and orthogonal to the key. In the fast-weight view, a key-directed delta edit cannot change the key-orthogonal part of a read. It uses the editable part to cancel old-state content measured along the query. It also improves retrieval at every length past the training window, and it about doubles the usable context length on S-NIAH-1.

</details>


### [217] [From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13675)
*Pranav Kumar Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2018年10月至2026年7月间AI模型从BERT等简单系统演进为能够解决复杂数学问题、编写软件的智能体的过程。真实编码问题解决能力自2024年底起每年提升近6倍，成本大幅下降。顶级性能分散于多个专业化模型：Claude Opus 5擅长前端编码，Claude Fable 5擅长仓库级编码，GPT 5.6 Sol擅长终端任务。在测试中，Qwen 2.5模型通过高级采样方法将小学算术测试成绩从58/100提升至79/100，置信度排序工具在前50个选择中正确识别47个正确答案，所有研究材料均已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跟踪并量化AI模型在编码能力、成本效率和专业化分工三个维度上的快速演进，为研究社区提供系统的性能基准和工具验证。

Method: 通过时间序列对比（2018-2026年）评估模型能力进步，采用成本-性能分析工具比较不同模型的经济性，使用Qwen 2.5进行小学算术测试，配合基础方法与高级采样方法的对照实验，并开发置信度排序工具用于答案筛选。

Result: 真实编码问题解决能力自2024年底起每年提升近6倍；GPT 5.6 Luna以每百万token 1-6美元的成本匹配旗舰性能；各专用模型分别在特定任务域领先；高级采样将数学测试准确率从58%提升至79%；置信度排序工具在top 50中正确识别47个答案。

Conclusion: AI编码能力正以指数级速度提升且成本持续下降，性能从通用型向专业化分工转变，采样策略和置信度排序等后处理技术能显著提升模型在推理任务中的表现。

Abstract: Between October 2018 and July 2026 AI models progressed from simple systems like BERT to massive agents that solve complex math and write software. The ability to resolve real coding issues improved by nearly six times per year since late 2024. During this time costs dropped sharply with OpenAIs budget model GPT 5 point 6 Luna matching flagship capabilities for just one to six dollars per million tokens beating older versions at a fraction of the price. Top performance is now split across specialized models as Claude Opus 5 leads in frontend coding Claude Fable 5 excels at repository level coding and GPT 5 point 6 Sol dominates terminal tasks. In a grade school math test using the Qwen 2 point 5 model basic methods solved 58 of 100 problems while advanced sampling solved up to 79. A confidence ranking tool correctly identified 47 right answers in its top 50 choices proving highly useful for sorting tasks with all research materials made fully public.

</details>


### [218] [EEG-PRISM: Physiologically-Grounded Interpretability of Predictions by EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13676)
*Deeksha M Shama,Punnisa Amornsirikul,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-PRISM，一种通用的EEG基础模型事后可解释性方法，通过线性变换和反向传播规则将时间-通道维度的归因分数映射到频率域（通过可逆DFT）和源域（通过近似可逆的EEG生成模型），无需修改或重训练底层模型，实现了符合临床直觉的频谱和空间域解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型是AI在脑电分析中的下一代进展，但现有可解释AI方法仅在时间-通道输入空间提供归因分数，与临床医生对EEG的直觉理解（如频段、脑区）不匹配。因此迫切需要一种通用方法，能够将任意基础模型的可解释性扩展到其他生理相关的域，而不需修改或重训练底层模型。

Method: EEG-PRISM利用线性变换和已有的反向传播规则，将时间-通道归因分数映射到替代域。具体包括：(1)通过可逆离散傅里叶变换(DFT)推导到频率域的映射；(2)通过近似可逆的EEG生成模型推导到源域的映射。评估涵盖模拟数据和真实数据，使用五个基础模型和四种AI解释器，检验在各域中恢复真实ground-truth现象的能力。

Result: 模拟实验中，EEG-PRISM实现了接近完美的频谱恢复和69.2%的空间准确率。在癫痫数据中，正确判断delta-theta活动最为显著，并以50%准确率正确定位癫痫发作起始区域。在自闭症数据中，将预测性的delta-alpha生物标志物定位到额叶和颞叶区域，与先前研究一致。

Conclusion: EEG-PRISM是一种理论上严谨的事后归因方法，能准确映射到频谱域和空间域。它支持对瞬态事件（如癫痫发作）的窗口级分析以及临床相关生物标志物（如自闭症）的群体级识别，从而推进了可解释EEG基础模型的发展。这项工作使EEG基础模型的解释具备生理学依据，并支持事件定位和生物标志物识别等临床洞察。

Abstract: Objective: Foundation models represent the next advancement in AI for EEG analysis; however current explainable AI techniques provide attribution scores in the time-channel input space, which is mismatched to clinical intuition about EEG. Thus, there is a critical need for a universal method that can extend the interpretability of any foundation model to alternative and physiologically relevant domains without modifying or retraining the underlying model. Methods: EEG-PRISM leverages linear transformations and established backpropagation rules to map time-channel attribution scores into alternative domains. We derive mappings to the frequency domain via an invertible DFT and to the source domain via an approximately invertible EEG generative model. We evaluate EEG-PRISM in simulated and real data, assessing recovery of ground-truth phenomena across domains with five foundation models and four AI explainers. Results: In simulation, EEG-PRISM achieves near-perfect spectral recovery and 69.2% spatial accuracy. In epilepsy, EEG-PRISM correctly determines that delta-theta activity is most salient and correctly localizes the seizure onset region with 50% accuracy. In autism, EEG-PRISM localizes the predictive delta-alpha biomarkers to frontal and temporal regions, consistent with prior work. Conclusion: EEG-PRISM is a theoretically-grounded post-hoc attribution method with accurate mapping into the spectral and spatial domains. It supports window-level analysis of transient events (e.g., seizures) and group-level identification of clinically relevant biomarkers (e.g., autism), thus advancing interpretable EEG foundation models. Significance: This work enables physiologically-grounded interpretation of EEG foundation models and supports clinically relevant insights such as event localization and biomarker identification.

</details>


### [219] [SAGE: Surrogate-gradient Adaptation via Attention-Guided Entropy for Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13702)
*Kiran Nair,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE方法，通过归一化自注意力熵估计块级不确定性，并据此在训练期间自适应调整代理梯度斜率，用于Transformer-based SNN的训练优化，在CIFAR-10/100上获得1-2%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）以其稀疏事件驱动计算成为节能的替代方案，但其训练面临挑战：脉冲函数的不可微性要求使用代理梯度，而固定形状的代理梯度在不同层和不同训练阶段可能并非最优，需要一种自适应机制来提升优化灵活性。

Method: 提出SAGE（Uncertainty-modulated Surrogate-gradient）机制：从归一化自注意力熵中估计块级不确定性，利用该信号在训练期间动态调整代理梯度斜率，推理阶段模型保持不变，仅调制训练期的代理参数，不引入额外部署成本。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100数据集上，SAGE相比固定代理梯度基线方法在多个仿真时间步上实现了一致的精度提升，收益达1-2%。

Conclusion: 注意力衍生的不确定性可作为轻量级训练信号，用于Transformer-based SNN的自适应代理梯度学习，展示了在保持架构和部署成本不变的前提下提升优化性能的潜力。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer an energy-efficient alternative to conventional deep neural networks by exploiting sparse event-driven computation, but their training remains challenging because the non-differentiable spike function requires surrogate gradients whose fixed shape may be suboptimal across layers and training stages. In this work, we introduce SAGE, an uncertainty-modulated surrogate-gradient mechanism for Transformer-based SNNs. SAGE estimates block-level uncertainty from normalized self-attention entropy and uses this signal to adapt the surrogate-gradient slope during training while leaving the inference model unchanged. By modulating only the training-time surrogate parameter, the proposed method preserves the original architecture and deployment cost while improving optimization flexibility. Experiments on CIFAR-10/100 demonstrate that SAGE achieves improved accuracy over fixed-surrogate baselines, with results up to 1-2\% consistent gains across multiple simulation time steps. These results highlight the potential of attention-derived uncertainty as a lightweight training signal for adaptive surrogate-gradient learning in transformer-based SNNs.

</details>


### [220] [Sequence prediction under a lying oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14102)
*Puspabeethi Samanta,Nikhil Karamchandani,Jayakrishnan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在m元序列上的序贯预测问题，每个时间步中学习者需在未知环境结果的情况下选择概率分布，并承担与所分配概率相关的代价。该代价函数刻画了通过"说谎预言机"的对比查询进行预测的复杂性，文中同时考虑了随机与对抗两种环境，为两种设置分别提出了算法，并建立了后悔值的对数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序贯预测通常假设代价函数是简单的（如对数损失），而本文考虑一种新兴场景：预测通过向可能说谎的预言机提出对比查询来实现，这引入了更复杂的代价结构。需要为这种新颖且实际重要的预测框架设计高效算法并分析其理论保障。

Method: 针对随机环境和对抗环境两种设置分别设计预测算法。算法的核心在于利用代价函数的特定结构（与通过说谎预言机进行对比查询的预测复杂性相关），以在线学习框架下的指数权重或类似策略进行概率分布更新，并分析其后悔界。

Result: 在随机环境下和对抗环境下提出的算法均建立了关于后悔值的对数上界（logarithmic upper bounds），证明了算法在两种不同环境设置下都能实现高效学习。

Conclusion: 本文为基于说谎预言机对比查询的序贯预测问题提供了统一的理论框架，在随机和对抗两种环境下均实现了对数后悔界，展示了该预测范式在理论上可行且高效。
